function getPalette(section){
  return PALETTES[section] || PALETTES.visao;
}
function applyLegendPalette(section){
  const palette = getPalette(section);
  const legend = document.querySelector(`#section-${section} .legend-scale`);
  if(!legend) return;
  [...legend.querySelectorAll('span')].forEach((el, i) => {
    el.style.background = palette[i] || palette[palette.length - 1];
  });
}

function fmt(v, kind){
  if(v === null || v === undefined || Number.isNaN(v)) return 'Sem dado';
  if(kind === 'pct') return `${(v*100).toLocaleString('pt-BR',{minimumFractionDigits:1, maximumFractionDigits:1})}%`;
  if(kind === 'money') return `${v.toLocaleString('pt-BR',{style:'currency',currency:'BRL',maximumFractionDigits:0})}`;
  if(kind === 'index') return v.toLocaleString('pt-BR',{minimumFractionDigits:2, maximumFractionDigits:2});
  if(kind === 'float1') return v.toLocaleString('pt-BR',{minimumFractionDigits:1, maximumFractionDigits:1});
  return v.toLocaleString('pt-BR');
}
function normalize(str){
  return str.normalize('NFD').replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g,'').toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g,' ').trim();
}
function canonical(name){
  const n = normalize(name || '');
  if(n === 'lourdes') return 'de lourdes';
  if(n === 'praia futuro i' || n === 'praia do futuro i') return 'praia futuro i';
  if(n === 'praia futuro ii' || n === 'praia do futuro ii') return 'praia futuro ii';
  if(n === 'praia futuro 1' || n === 'praia do futuro 1' || n === 'praia do futuro') return 'praia futuro i';
  if(n === 'praia futuro 2' || n === 'praia do futuro 2') return 'praia futuro ii';
  if(n === 'vicente pinzon' || n === 'vicente pinzon') return 'vicente pinzon';
  return n;
}

const OSC_THEME_METRIC_LABELS = {
  oscGeoCount:'OSCs ativas geolocalizadas',
  oscGeo10k:'OSCs ativas geolocalizadas por 10 mil hab.',
  oscGeoShare:'Participação das OSCs filtradas no total ativo do bairro'
};
function escHtml(value){
  return String(value ?? '').replace(/[&<>"']/g, ch => ({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[ch]));
}
function formatCNPJ(value){
  const digits = String(value || '').replace(/\D/g,'');
  if(digits.length !== 14) return value || 'Sem dado';
  return `${digits.slice(0,2)}.${digits.slice(2,5)}.${digits.slice(5,8)}/${digits.slice(8,12)}-${digits.slice(12)}`;
}
function oscColor(point){
  return OSC_CATEGORY_COLORS[point.primary] || OSC_CATEGORY_COLORS['Sem classificação'];
}
function normalizeOscFilterSelection(values, options){
  const validOptions = (options || []).filter(o => o !== 'Todos');
  const validValues = [...new Set((values || []).filter(v => validOptions.includes(v)))];
  return !validValues.length || validValues.length === validOptions.length ? ['Todos'] : validValues;
}
function getMultiFilterValues(key){
  const panel = document.getElementById(`${key}-panel`);
  if(!panel) return ['Todos'];
  const checked = [...panel.querySelectorAll('input[type="checkbox"]:checked')].map(input => input.value);
  const options = [...panel.querySelectorAll('input[type="checkbox"]')].map(input => input.value);
  return normalizeOscFilterSelection(checked, ['Todos', ...options]);
}
function formatOscFilterLabel(values, allLabel, countLabel){
  const selected = (values || []).filter(v => v !== 'Todos');
  if(!selected.length) return allLabel;
  if(selected.length === 1) return selected[0];
  if(selected.length === 2) return `${selected[0]} +1`;
  return `${selected.length} ${countLabel}`;
}
function getOscFilterState(){
  return {
    areas: getMultiFilterValues('osc-area'),
    subareas: getMultiFilterValues('osc-subarea')
  };
}
function getOscFilterSummary(state = getOscFilterState()){
  return {
    areaLabel: formatOscFilterLabel(state.areas, 'Todas as áreas', 'áreas'),
    subareaLabel: formatOscFilterLabel(state.subareas, 'Todas as subáreas', 'subáreas'),
    hasAreaFilter: !state.areas.includes('Todos'),
    hasSubareaFilter: !state.subareas.includes('Todos')
  };
}
function renderMultiFilter(key, options, selectedValues, allLabel, countLabel){
  const panel = document.getElementById(`${key}-panel`);
  const button = document.getElementById(`${key}-btn`);
  if(!panel || !button) return;
  const validOptions = options.filter(o => o !== 'Todos');
  const normalized = normalizeOscFilterSelection(selectedValues, options);
  const selectedSet = new Set(normalized.includes('Todos') ? validOptions : normalized);
  panel.innerHTML = `
    <div class="filter-panel-actions">
      <button type="button" class="filter-panel-action" data-filter-key="${key}" data-filter-action="all">Todos</button>
      <button type="button" class="filter-panel-action" data-filter-key="${key}" data-filter-action="clear">Limpar</button>
    </div>
    ${validOptions.map(option => `
      <label class="filter-option">
        <input type="checkbox" class="filter-option-input" data-filter-key="${key}" value="${escHtml(option)}" ${selectedSet.has(option) ? 'checked' : ''}>
        <span class="filter-option-text">${escHtml(option)}</span>
      </label>
    `).join('')}
  `;
  button.textContent = formatOscFilterLabel(normalized, allLabel, countLabel);
}
function pointMatchesOscFilters(point, areas=['Todos'], subareas=['Todos']){
  const pointAreas = point.areas?.length ? point.areas : ['Sem classificação'];
  const pointSubs = point.subs?.length ? point.subs : [];
  const allAreas = !areas?.length || areas.includes('Todos');
  const allSubs = !subareas?.length || subareas.includes('Todos');
  const areaOk = allAreas || areas.some(area => pointAreas.includes(area));
  const subOk = allSubs || subareas.some(subarea => pointSubs.includes(subarea));
  return areaOk && subOk;
}
function getOscPointsFiltered(territory, bairro, areas=['Todos'], subareas=['Todos']){
  return OSC_POINTS.filter(p =>
    (territory === 'Todos' || p.t === territory) &&
    (bairro === 'Todos' || p.b === bairro) &&
    pointMatchesOscFilters(p, areas, subareas)
  );
}
function getOscAreaOptions(territory='Todos', bairro='Todos'){
  const areas = new Set();
  getOscPointsFiltered(territory, bairro, ['Todos'], ['Todos']).forEach(point => {
    (point.areas?.length ? point.areas : ['Sem classificação']).forEach(area => areas.add(area));
  });
  return ['Todos', ...[...areas].sort((a,b)=>a.localeCompare(b,'pt-BR'))];
}
function getOscSubareaOptions(territory='Todos', bairro='Todos', areas=['Todos']){
  const subs = new Set();
  getOscPointsFiltered(territory, bairro, areas, ['Todos']).forEach(point => {
    (point.subs?.length ? point.subs : []).forEach(sub => subs.add(sub));
  });
  return ['Todos', ...[...subs].sort((a,b)=>a.localeCompare(b,'pt-BR'))];
}
function syncOscFilterControls(territory, bairro){
  const areaOptions = getOscAreaOptions(territory, bairro);
  const currentAreas = getMultiFilterValues('osc-area');
  const selectedAreas = normalizeOscFilterSelection(currentAreas, areaOptions);
  renderMultiFilter('osc-area', areaOptions, selectedAreas, 'Todas as áreas', 'áreas');
  const subOptions = getOscSubareaOptions(territory, bairro, selectedAreas);
  const currentSubareas = getMultiFilterValues('osc-subarea');
  const selectedSubareas = normalizeOscFilterSelection(currentSubareas, subOptions);
  renderMultiFilter('osc-subarea', subOptions, selectedSubareas, 'Todas as subáreas', 'subáreas');
}
function getOscMetricLabel(metric, areas=['Todos'], subareas=['Todos']){
  const base = OSC_THEME_METRIC_LABELS[metric] || METRICS[metric]?.label || 'Indicador';
  const areaSummary = getOscFilterSummary({areas, subareas});
  if(areaSummary.hasSubareaFilter) return `${base} · ${areaSummary.subareaLabel}`;
  if(areaSummary.hasAreaFilter) return `${base} · ${areaSummary.areaLabel}`;
  return base;
}
function getMetricLabel(section, metric){
  if(section === 'osc'){
    const {areas, subareas} = getOscFilterState();
    return getOscMetricLabel(metric, areas, subareas);
  }
  return METRICS[metric].label;
}
function findOscTopArea(points){
  if(!points.length) return 'Sem OSC filtrada';
  const counts = {};
  points.forEach(point => {
    const key = point.primary || 'Sem classificação';
    counts[key] = (counts[key] || 0) + 1;
  });
  return Object.entries(counts).sort((a,b) => b[1] - a[1])[0]?.[0] || 'Sem classificação';
}
function getOscAggregateRows(territory='Todos', bairro='Todos', areas=['Todos'], subareas=['Todos']){
  const scopeRows = DATA.filter(d => territory === 'Todos' || d.t === territory);
  return scopeRows
    .map(d => {
      const allPoints = OSC_POINTS.filter(p => p.t === d.t && p.b === d.b);
      const filteredPoints = allPoints.filter(p => pointMatchesOscFilters(p, areas, subareas));
      const distinctAreas = new Set(filteredPoints.flatMap(point => point.areas?.length ? point.areas : ['Sem classificação']));
      const distinctSubs = new Set(filteredPoints.flatMap(point => point.subs?.length ? point.subs : []));
      return {
        ...d,
        oscGeoCount: filteredPoints.length,
        oscGeoTotal: allPoints.length,
        oscGeo10k: d.pop ? filteredPoints.length / d.pop * 10000 : 0,
        oscGeoShare: allPoints.length ? filteredPoints.length / allPoints.length : 0,
        oscGeoAreasDistinct: distinctAreas.size,
        oscGeoSubsDistinct: distinctSubs.size,
        oscGeoTopArea: findOscTopArea(filteredPoints)
      };
    })
    .filter(d => bairro === 'Todos' || d.b === bairro);
}
function buildOscPopup(point){
  const areas = point.areas?.length ? point.areas : ['Sem classificação'];
  const subs = point.subs?.length ? point.subs : [];
  const chips = areas.map(area => {
    const color = OSC_CATEGORY_COLORS[area] || OSC_CATEGORY_COLORS['Sem classificação'];
    return `<span class="chip"><span class="chip-dot" style="background:${color}"></span>${escHtml(area)}</span>`;
  }).join('');
  const subchips = subs.length
    ? subs.map(sub => `<span class="chip chip-muted">${escHtml(sub)}</span>`).join('')
    : '<span class="chip chip-muted">Sem subárea detalhada</span>';
  return `
    <div class="osc-popup-card">
      <div class="osc-popup-head">
        <div class="popup-kicker">OSC ativa</div>
        <div class="popup-title">${escHtml(point.n)}</div>
        <div class="popup-ter">${escHtml(point.b)} · ${escHtml(point.t)}</div>
      </div>
      <div class="osc-popup-body">
        <div class="popup-grid">
          <div class="popup-stat"><strong>${escHtml(point.primary)}</strong><span>Perfil principal</span></div>
          <div class="popup-stat"><strong>${areas.length}</strong><span>Frentes de atuação</span></div>
          <div class="popup-stat"><strong>${escHtml(point.tipo || 'Sem dado')}</strong><span>Matriz / filial</span></div>
          <div class="popup-stat"><strong>${formatCNPJ(point.id)}</strong><span>CNPJ</span></div>
        </div>
        ${point.rf ? `<div class="osc-popup-meta"><strong>Razão social:</strong> ${escHtml(point.rf)}</div>` : ''}
        <div class="osc-popup-meta"><strong>Endereço cadastrado:</strong> ${escHtml(point.addr || 'Sem dado')}</div>
        <div class="osc-popup-block">
          <span class="osc-popup-label">Áreas de atuação</span>
          <div class="chip-row">${chips}</div>
        </div>
        <div class="osc-popup-block">
          <span class="osc-popup-label">Subáreas</span>
          <div class="chip-row">${subchips}</div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  `;
}
function renderOscPointLayer(state, territory, bairro, areas=['Todos'], subareas=['Todos']){
  if(!state.oscPointLayer) return {count:0, shifted:0};
  state.oscPointLayer.clearLayers();
  const points = getOscPointsFiltered(territory, bairro, areas, subareas);
  const radius = bairro !== 'Todos' ? 6.8 : territory !== 'Todos' ? 6 : 5.4;
  points.forEach(point => {
    const color = oscColor(point);
    const marker = L.circleMarker([point.plat, point.plon], {
      pane:'points',
      radius,
      color:'#ffffff',
      weight:1.4,
      fillColor:color,
      fillOpacity:0.92,
      bubblingMouseEvents:false
    });
    marker.bindPopup(buildOscPopup(point), {maxWidth:340});
    state.oscPointLayer.addLayer(marker);
  });
  return {count:points.length, shifted:points.filter(p => p.shift).length};
}

function findDataByFeature(feature){
  const key = canonical(feature.properties.nome);
  return LOOKUP[key] || null;
}
function getBairros(territory){
  const arr = DATA.filter(d => territory === 'Todos' || d.t === territory).map(d => d.b).sort((a,b)=>a.localeCompare(b,'pt-BR'));
  return ['Todos', ...arr];
}
function calcBreaks(values, steps=6){
  const clean = values.filter(v => Number.isFinite(v));
  if(!clean.length) return [0,1];
  const min = Math.min(...clean);
  const max = Math.max(...clean);
  if(min === max) return [min, max];
  const out = [];
  for(let i=0;i<=steps;i++) out.push(min + (max-min)*(i/steps));
  return out;
}
function getColor(value, breaks, section){
  const palette = getPalette(section);
  if(value === null || value === undefined || Number.isNaN(value)) return '#edf2f7';
  const min = breaks[0], max = breaks[breaks.length-1];
  if(max === min) return palette[palette.length-1];
  const ratio = Math.min(0.999999, Math.max(0, (value-min)/(max-min)));
  const idx = Math.min(palette.length-1, Math.floor(ratio * palette.length));
  return palette[idx];
}
function filteredData(territory, bairro){
  return DATA.filter(d => (territory === 'Todos' || d.t === territory) && (bairro === 'Todos' || d.b === bairro));
}
function buildPopup(d, metric, section='visao'){
  if(!d) return '<div class="popup-title">Bairro sem base analítica nesta versão</div>';
  const metricLabel = getMetricLabel(section, metric);
  if(section === 'osc'){
    const oscFilterState = getOscFilterState();
    const oscFilterSummary = getOscFilterSummary(oscFilterState);
    const thematicFilter = oscFilterSummary.hasSubareaFilter
      ? `Subáreas selecionadas: ${oscFilterSummary.subareaLabel}`
      : oscFilterSummary.hasAreaFilter
        ? `Áreas selecionadas: ${oscFilterSummary.areaLabel}`
        : 'Sem filtro temático adicional';
    return `
      <div class="popup-title">${d.b}</div>
      <div class="popup-ter">${d.t} · Base ativa geolocalizada</div>
      <div class="popup-grid">
        <div class="popup-stat"><strong>${fmt(d[metric], METRICS[metric].fmt)}</strong><span>${metricLabel}</span></div>
        <div class="popup-stat"><strong>${fmt(d.oscGeoTotal, 'int')}</strong><span>OSCs ativas no bairro</span></div>
        <div class="popup-stat"><strong>${fmt(d.oscGeo10k, 'float1')}</strong><span>Ativas por 10 mil hab.</span></div>
        <div class="popup-stat"><strong>${fmt(d.oscGeoShare, 'pct')}</strong><span>Peso do filtro</span></div>
      </div>
      <div class="hr"></div>
      <div class="small">${escHtml(thematicFilter)} · Perfil predominante no recorte: ${escHtml(d.oscGeoTopArea || 'Sem classificação')}</div>
      <div class="small">Áreas distintas no resultado: ${fmt(d.oscGeoAreasDistinct, 'int')} · Subáreas distintas: ${fmt(d.oscGeoSubsDistinct, 'int')}</div>
    `;
  }
  return `<div class="popup-card"><h4>${d.b}</h4>
    <div style="font-size:11px;color:var(--muted);margin-bottom:6px">${d.t}${d.idhClass ? ' · IDH '+d.idhClass : ''}${d.favelas ? ' · Com favelas' : ''}</div>
    <div class="popup-grid">
      <div class="popup-stat"><strong>${fmt(d.pop,'int')}</strong><span>População</span></div>
      <div class="popup-stat"><strong>${fmt(d.epob, 'pct')}</strong><span>Ext. pobreza</span></div>
      <div class="popup-stat"><strong>${fmt(d.rendaPC, 'money')}</strong><span>Rend. resp. per capita</span></div>
      <div class="popup-stat"><strong>${fmt(d.renda, 'money')}</strong><span>Renda responsável</span></div>
      <div class="popup-stat"><strong>${fmt(d.idhm, 'index')}</strong><span>IDHM</span></div>
      <div class="popup-stat"><strong>${fmt(d.esg, 'pct')}</strong><span>Esgoto</span></div>
      <div class="popup-stat"><strong>${fmt(d.emp, 'int')}</strong><span>Empresas ativas</span></div>
      <div class="popup-stat"><strong>${fmt(d.osc, 'int')}</strong><span>OSCs totais</span></div>
    </div>
    <div class="popup-footer">CadÚnico: ${fmt(d.cad,'pct')} · IBEU: ${fmt(d.ibeu,'index')} · Neg. impacto: ${fmt(d.negImpacto,'int')} · Grav. adolesc.: ${fmt(d.gravAdol,'float1')}%</div>
  </div>
  `;
}

function fillSelect(selectId, options, selected, allLabel='Todos'){
  const el = document.getElementById(selectId);
  el.innerHTML = options.map(o => `<option value="${o}" ${o===selected?'selected':''}>${o === 'Todos' ? allLabel : o}</option>`).join('');
}

function mean(rows, key){
  const vals = rows.map(d => d[key]).filter(v => Number.isFinite(v));
  if(!vals.length) return null;
  return vals.reduce((acc,v)=>acc+v,0) / vals.length;
}
function median(rows, key){
  const vals = rows.map(d => d[key]).filter(v => Number.isFinite(v)).sort((a,b)=>a-b);
  if(!vals.length) return null;
  const mid = Math.floor(vals.length/2);
  return vals.length % 2 ? vals[mid] : (vals[mid-1] + vals[mid]) / 2;
}
function signedDelta(value, reference, kind){
  if(!Number.isFinite(value) || !Number.isFinite(reference)) return 'Sem comparação';
  const diff = value - reference;
  const sign = diff > 0 ? '+' : '';
  if(kind === 'pct') return `${sign}${(diff*100).toLocaleString('pt-BR',{minimumFractionDigits:1, maximumFractionDigits:1})} p.p.`;
  if(kind === 'index') return `${sign}${diff.toLocaleString('pt-BR',{minimumFractionDigits:2, maximumFractionDigits:2})}`;
  if(kind === 'money') return `${sign}${diff.toLocaleString('pt-BR',{style:'currency',currency:'BRL',maximumFractionDigits:0})}`;
  if(kind === 'float1') return `${sign}${diff.toLocaleString('pt-BR',{minimumFractionDigits:1, maximumFractionDigits:1})}`;
  return `${sign}${diff.toLocaleString('pt-BR')}`;
}
function scopeLabel(territory, bairro){
  if(bairro !== 'Todos') return bairro;
  if(territory !== 'Todos') return territory;
  return 'Fortaleza nos bairros com base atual';
}
function buildStrategyText(section, metric, ctx){
  const {territory, bairro, top, bottom, averageValue, cityAverage, selected, count} = ctx;
  const scope = scopeLabel(territory, bairro);
  if(section === 'demo'){
    if(metric === 'dens') return bairro !== 'Todos'
      ? `Isso sugere um padrão local de pressão urbana que precisa ser lido em conjunto com a ocupação do entorno imediato.`
      : `A distribuição de densidade ajuda a separar áreas de maior pressão urbana de áreas menos adensadas dentro de ${scope}.`;
    if(metric === 'pop') return `O mapa evidencia onde a escala populacional tende a concentrar maior demanda potencial por serviços e articulação territorial em ${scope}.`;
    return `A leitura demográfica ajuda a distinguir bairros mais intensivos em composição social e ocupação do espaço dentro de ${scope}.`;
  }
  if(section === 'vuln'){
    if(metric === 'idhm') return `Como o IDHM é um indicador-síntese de desenvolvimento, o contraste espacial ajuda a localizar bolsões de menor bem-estar relativo dentro de ${scope}.`;
    return `Nesse recorte, o mapa reforça onde a vulnerabilidade social aparece de forma mais concentrada e onde a resposta tende a exigir maior focalização.`;
  }
  if(section === 'infra'){
    if(metric === 'esc') return `A quantidade de escolas funciona como proxy de capilaridade de equipamentos, mas deve ser lida junto com pressão demográfica e cobertura de serviços.`;
    if(metric === 'esfc') return `A cobertura de eSF ajuda a inferir capacidade de atenção básica e proximidade da rede pública nos bairros selecionados.`;
    return `A infraestrutura aparece como condicionante de implementação: valores mais baixos tendem a sinalizar maior dependência de coordenação com serviços públicos locais.`;
  }
  if(section === 'econ'){
    if(metric === 'meiShare') return `Uma participação elevada de MEIs sugere base produtiva mais pulverizada e possivelmente mais aderente a estratégias de pequenos negócios.`;
    if(metric === 'renda') return `A renda ajuda a distinguir bairros com maior tração econômica daqueles em que a inclusão produtiva depende mais de ativação de oportunidades.`;
    return `O mapa mostra onde a tração econômica já está mais presente e onde a densidade empresarial ainda é mais restrita.`;
  }
  if(section === 'osc'){
    const oscFilterSummary = getOscFilterSummary(getOscFilterState());
    if(metric === 'oscGeoShare'){
      return oscFilterSummary.hasSubareaFilter || oscFilterSummary.hasAreaFilter
        ? `Esse indicador mostra onde o perfil filtrado tem maior peso relativo dentro do mix local de organizações, evitando que o volume absoluto esconda especializações temáticas.`
        : `Sem filtro temático adicional, a participação tende a ser uniforme; use área ou subárea para revelar especializações relativas por bairro.`;
    }
    if(oscFilterSummary.hasSubareaFilter || oscFilterSummary.hasAreaFilter){
      return `Com filtro temático aplicado, o mapa mostra onde o perfil selecionado se concentra e onde a presença ainda é rarefeita dentro do ecossistema local.`;
    }
    return `A distribuição institucional permite enxergar onde o ecossistema social ativo já é mais denso e onde a presença de organizações permanece mais limitada.`;
  }
  return `O recorte de ${scope} mostra contrastes internos relevantes para a leitura territorial.`;
}
function renderMapAnalysis(section, territory, bairro, metric, rows){
  const titleEl = document.getElementById(`map-summary-title-${section}`);
  const textEl = document.getElementById(`map-summary-${section}`);
  const insightsEl = document.getElementById(`map-insights-${section}`);
  if(!titleEl || !textEl || !insightsEl) return;

  const metricLabel = getMetricLabel(section, metric);
  const validRows = rows.filter(d => Number.isFinite(d[metric]));
  if(!validRows.length){
    titleEl.textContent = `${metricLabel} · ${scopeLabel(territory, bairro)}`;
    textEl.textContent = section === 'osc'
      ? 'Não há OSCs ativas geolocalizadas suficientes para produzir leitura descritiva com a combinação atual de filtros territoriais e temáticos.'
      : 'Não há dados suficientes para produzir uma leitura descritiva com a combinação atual de filtros.';
    insightsEl.innerHTML = '';
    return;
  }

  const sorted = [...validRows].sort((a,b)=>b[metric]-a[metric]);
  const top = sorted[0];
  const bottom = sorted[sorted.length - 1];
  const selected = bairro !== 'Todos' ? validRows.find(d => d.b === bairro) || validRows[0] : null;

  const comparisonRows = section === 'osc'
    ? (() => { const s = getOscFilterState(); return getOscAggregateRows(territory, 'Todos', s.areas, s.subareas); })()
    : DATA.filter(d => (territory === 'Todos' ? true : d.t === territory) && Number.isFinite(d[metric]));
  const cityRows = section === 'osc'
    ? (() => { const s = getOscFilterState(); return getOscAggregateRows('Todos', 'Todos', s.areas, s.subareas); })()
    : DATA.filter(d => Number.isFinite(d[metric]));

  const averageValue = mean(validRows, metric);
  const territoryAverage = mean(comparisonRows, metric);
  const cityAverage = mean(cityRows, metric);
  const med = median(validRows, metric);
  const amplitude = Number.isFinite(top?.[metric]) && Number.isFinite(bottom?.[metric]) ? top[metric] - bottom[metric] : null;

  titleEl.textContent = `${metricLabel} · ${scopeLabel(territory, bairro)}`;

  let summary = '';
  if(selected && Number.isFinite(selected[metric])){
    const refBase = territory !== 'Todos' ? territoryAverage : cityAverage;
    const refLabel = territory !== 'Todos' ? `a média de ${territory}` : 'a média dos bairros com base atual';
    summary = `${selected.b} registra <strong>${fmt(selected[metric], METRICS[metric].fmt)}</strong> em ${metricLabel}. No mesmo recorte comparável, ${refLabel} é <strong>${fmt(refBase, METRICS[metric].fmt)}</strong>, o que representa uma diferença de <strong>${signedDelta(selected[metric], refBase, METRICS[metric].fmt)}</strong>. ${buildStrategyText(section, metric, {territory, bairro, top, bottom, averageValue, cityAverage, selected, count:validRows.length})}`;
  } else {
    summary = `No recorte atual, <strong>${top.b}</strong> apresenta o maior valor em ${metricLabel} (<strong>${fmt(top[metric], METRICS[metric].fmt)}</strong>) e <strong>${bottom.b}</strong> o menor (<strong>${fmt(bottom[metric], METRICS[metric].fmt)}</strong>). A média do conjunto é <strong>${fmt(averageValue, METRICS[metric].fmt)}</strong> e a mediana é <strong>${fmt(med, METRICS[metric].fmt)}</strong>. ${buildStrategyText(section, metric, {territory, bairro, top, bottom, averageValue, cityAverage, selected, count:validRows.length})}`;
  }
  textEl.innerHTML = summary;

  const cards = [];
  cards.push({
    label:'Maior valor no recorte',
    value:fmt(top[metric], METRICS[metric].fmt),
    sub:top.b
  });
  cards.push({
    label:'Menor valor no recorte',
    value:fmt(bottom[metric], METRICS[metric].fmt),
    sub:bottom.b
  });
  cards.push({
    label:'Média do recorte',
    value:fmt(averageValue, METRICS[metric].fmt),
    sub:`Base comparável: ${validRows.length} bairro(s)`
  });
  cards.push({
    label:selected ? 'Diferença para a referência' : 'Amplitude interna',
    value:selected && Number.isFinite(selected[metric]) ? signedDelta(selected[metric], territory !== 'Todos' ? territoryAverage : cityAverage, METRICS[metric].fmt) : fmt(amplitude, METRICS[metric].fmt),
    sub:selected && Number.isFinite(selected[metric])
      ? (territory !== 'Todos' ? `vs. média de ${territory}` : 'vs. média da base atual')
      : 'Distância entre o maior e o menor valor'
  });

  insightsEl.innerHTML = cards.map(card => `
    <div class="analysis-stat">
      <div class="analysis-stat-label">${card.label}</div>
      <div class="analysis-stat-value">${card.value}</div>
      <div class="analysis-stat-sub">${card.sub}</div>
    </div>
  `).join('');
}

function setupOscFilterInteractions(){
  if(window.__oscFilterInteractionsBound) return;
  window.__oscFilterInteractionsBound = true;

  document.addEventListener('click', event => {
    const toggle = event.target.closest('[data-filter-toggle]');
    const action = event.target.closest('[data-filter-action]');
    const insidePopover = event.target.closest('.filter-popover');

    if(toggle){
      const popover = toggle.closest('.filter-popover');
      document.querySelectorAll('.filter-popover.open').forEach(el => {
        if(el !== popover) el.classList.remove('open');
      });
      popover?.classList.toggle('open');
      return;
    }

    if(action){
      const key = action.dataset.filterKey;
      const panel = document.getElementById(`${key}-panel`);
      if(!panel) return;
      const checks = [...panel.querySelectorAll('input[type="checkbox"]')];
      if(action.dataset.filterAction === 'all'){
        checks.forEach(input => input.checked = true);
      }else{
        checks.forEach(input => input.checked = false);
      }
      if(key === 'osc-area'){
        syncOscFilterControls(document.getElementById('osc-territorio-filter').value, document.getElementById('osc-bairro-filter').value);
      }
      updateMap('osc');
      return;
    }

    if(!insidePopover){
      document.querySelectorAll('.filter-popover.open').forEach(el => el.classList.remove('open'));
    }
  });

  document.addEventListener('change', event => {
    const input = event.target.closest('.filter-option-input');
    if(!input) return;
    const key = input.dataset.filterKey;
    if(key === 'osc-area'){
      syncOscFilterControls(document.getElementById('osc-territorio-filter').value, document.getElementById('osc-bairro-filter').value);
    }else{
      const state = getOscFilterState();
      const summary = getOscFilterSummary(state);
      const btn = document.getElementById(`${key}-btn`);
      if(btn){
        btn.textContent = key === 'osc-subarea' ? summary.subareaLabel : summary.areaLabel;
      }
    }
    updateMap('osc');
  });

  document.addEventListener('keydown', event => {
    if(event.key === 'Escape'){
      document.querySelectorAll('.filter-popover.open').forEach(el => el.classList.remove('open'));
    }
  });
}

function createMap(section){
  const cfg = MAP_CONFIG[section];
  fillSelect(`${section}-territorio`, TERRITORIES, cfg.defaults.territorio, 'Todos os territórios');
  fillSelect(`${section}-bairro`, getBairros(cfg.defaults.territorio), cfg.defaults.bairro, 'Todos os bairros');
  const metricEl = document.getElementById(`${section}-indicador`);
  metricEl.innerHTML = cfg.metrics.map(m => `<option value="${m}" ${m===cfg.defaults.indicador?'selected':''}>${METRICS[m].label}</option>`).join('');
  if(section === 'osc'){
    syncOscFilterControls(cfg.defaults.territorio, cfg.defaults.bairro);
  }

  const map = L.map(`map-${section}`, {
    zoomControl:false,
    attributionControl:true,
    preferCanvas:true,
    renderer:L.canvas({padding:0.5}),
    zoomSnap:0.25
  });
  map.attributionControl.setPrefix(false);
  map.createPane('basemap');
  map.getPane('basemap').style.zIndex = 200;
  map.getPane('basemap').style.opacity = 0.76;
  map.getPane('basemap').style.filter = 'grayscale(1) contrast(1.02) brightness(1.03)';
  map.createPane('fills');
  map.getPane('fills').style.zIndex = 320;
  map.createPane('outlines');
  map.getPane('outlines').style.zIndex = 360;
  map.createPane('focus');
  map.getPane('focus').style.zIndex = 390;
  if(section === 'osc'){
    map.createPane('points');
    map.getPane('points').style.zIndex = 420;
  }
  map.createPane('labels');
  map.getPane('labels').style.zIndex = 450;
  map.getPane('labels').style.pointerEvents = 'none';

  L.tileLayer('https://{s}.basemaps.cartocdn.com/light_nolabels/{z}/{x}/{y}{r}.png', {
    subdomains:'abcd',
    maxZoom:20,
    pane:'basemap',
    attribution:'&copy; OpenStreetMap contributors &copy; CARTO'
  }).addTo(map);

  L.tileLayer('https://{s}.basemaps.cartocdn.com/light_only_labels/{z}/{x}/{y}{r}.png', {
    subdomains:'abcd',
    maxZoom:20,
    pane:'labels',
    attribution:'&copy; OpenStreetMap contributors &copy; CARTO'
  }).addTo(map);
  L.control.zoom({position:'bottomleft'}).addTo(map);

  const fillGeo = L.geoJSON(GEOJSON, {
    pane:'fills',
    renderer:map.options.renderer,
    smoothFactor:0,
    style: () => ({
      stroke:false,
      fillColor:UI_COLORS.gray,
      fillOpacity:0.78
    }),
    onEachFeature:(feature, layer) => {
      layer.on('click', () => {
        const metric = document.getElementById(`${section}-indicador`).value;
        const d = section === 'osc'
          ? (MAPS[section]?.rowLookup?.[canonical(feature.properties.nome)] || null)
          : findDataByFeature(feature);
        layer.bindPopup(buildPopup(d, metric, section),{maxWidth:340}).openPopup();
      });
    }
  }).addTo(map);

  const boundaryGeo = L.geoJSON(UNIQUE_BOUNDARIES, {
    pane:'outlines',
    renderer:map.options.renderer,
    interactive:false,
    smoothFactor:0,
    style:() => ({
      color:'#c7cfd8',
      weight:1.05,
      opacity:1,
      fill:false,
      lineCap:'round',
      lineJoin:'round'
    })
  }).addTo(map);

  const focusGeo = L.geoJSON(GEOJSON, {
    pane:'focus',
    renderer:map.options.renderer,
    interactive:false,
    smoothFactor:0,
    style:() => ({
      color:'rgba(0,0,0,0)',
      weight:0,
      opacity:0,
      fill:false,
      lineCap:'round',
      lineJoin:'round'
    })
  }).addTo(map);

  const oscPointLayer = section === 'osc' ? L.featureGroup().addTo(map) : null;

  MAPS[section] = {map, fillGeo, boundaryGeo, focusGeo, bounds: fillGeo.getBounds(), oscPointLayer, rowLookup:{}};
  applyLegendPalette(section);
  updateMap(section);

  document.getElementById(`${section}-territorio`).addEventListener('change', e => {
    fillSelect(`${section}-bairro`, getBairros(e.target.value), 'Todos', 'Todos os bairros');
    if(section === 'osc'){
      syncOscFilterControls(e.target.value, 'Todos');
    }
    updateMap(section);
    if(section === 'visao') refreshOverviewCharts(e.target.value, 'Todos');
  });
  document.getElementById(`${section}-bairro`).addEventListener('change', () => {
    if(section === 'osc'){
      syncOscFilterControls(document.getElementById('osc-territorio-filter').value, document.getElementById('osc-bairro-filter').value);
    }
    updateMap(section);
    if(section === 'visao'){
      const t = document.getElementById('visao-territorio').value;
      const b = document.getElementById('visao-bairro').value;
      refreshOverviewCharts(t, b);
    }
  });
  document.getElementById(`${section}-indicador`).addEventListener('change', () => updateMap(section));

  if(section === 'osc'){
    setupOscFilterInteractions();
  }
}

function updateMap(section){
  let territory, bairro, metric;
  if(section === 'osc'){
    territory = (document.getElementById('osc-territorio-filter')||{}).value || 'Todos';
    bairro = (document.getElementById('osc-bairro-filter')||{}).value || 'Todos';
    metric = 'osc';
  } else {
    territory = document.getElementById(`${section}-territorio`).value;
    bairro = document.getElementById(`${section}-bairro`).value;
    metric = document.getElementById(`${section}-indicador`).value;
  }
  const state = MAPS[section];
  const oscFilters = section === 'osc' ? getOscFilterState() : null;
  const allRows = section === 'osc'
    ? getOscAggregateRows(territory, 'Todos', oscFilters.areas, oscFilters.subareas)
    : filteredData(territory, 'Todos');
  const rows = section === 'osc'
    ? (bairro === 'Todos' ? allRows : allRows.filter(d => d.b === bairro))
    : filteredData(territory, bairro);
  const rowLookup = Object.fromEntries(allRows.map(d => [canonical(d.b), d]));
  state.rowLookup = rowLookup;

  const values = rows.map(d => d[metric]).filter(v => Number.isFinite(v));
  const hasPositiveOscValues = section !== 'osc' || rows.some(d => Number.isFinite(d[metric]) && d[metric] > 0);
  const breaks = calcBreaks(section === 'osc' && hasPositiveOscValues ? values : values);
  const neutralGray = UI_COLORS.gray;
  const neutralGraySoft = UI_COLORS.graySoft;
  let focusLayers = [];
  const oscPointStats = section === 'osc' ? renderOscPointLayer(state, territory, bairro, oscFilters.areas, oscFilters.subareas) : null;

  state.fillGeo.eachLayer(layer => {
    const baseRow = findDataByFeature(layer.feature);
    const d = section === 'osc'
      ? rowLookup[canonical(layer.feature.properties.nome)] || null
      : baseRow;
    const inTerritory = d && (territory === 'Todos' || d.t === territory);
    const isSelected = inTerritory && (bairro === 'Todos' || d.b === bairro);
    const hasMetric = d && Number.isFinite(d[metric]);
    const value = d ? d[metric] : null;

    let fillColor = neutralGray;
    let fillOpacity = 0.76;

    const shouldColorOsc = section !== 'osc' || (hasPositiveOscValues && value > 0);

    if(territory === 'Todos' && bairro === 'Todos' && hasMetric && shouldColorOsc){
      fillColor = getColor(value, breaks, section);
      fillOpacity = 0.90;
      focusLayers.push(layer);
    } else if(inTerritory && bairro === 'Todos' && hasMetric && shouldColorOsc){
      fillColor = getColor(value, breaks, section);
      fillOpacity = 0.90;
      focusLayers.push(layer);
    } else if(isSelected && hasMetric && shouldColorOsc){
      fillColor = getColor(value, breaks, section);
      fillOpacity = 0.94;
      focusLayers.push(layer);
    } else {
      fillColor = d ? neutralGraySoft : neutralGray;
      fillOpacity = d ? 0.78 : 0.72;
    }

    layer.setStyle({fillColor, fillOpacity, stroke:false});
  });

  state.focusGeo.eachLayer(layer => {
    const d = section === 'osc'
      ? rowLookup[canonical(layer.feature.properties.nome)] || null
      : findDataByFeature(layer.feature);
    const inTerritory = d && (territory === 'Todos' || d.t === territory);
    const isSelected = inTerritory && (bairro === 'Todos' || d.b === bairro);

    let color = 'rgba(0,0,0,0)';
    let weight = 0;
    let opacity = 0;

    if(isSelected && bairro !== 'Todos'){
      color = UI_COLORS.redDark;
      weight = 2.2;
      opacity = 0.98;
      layer.bringToFront();
    }

    layer.setStyle({
      color,
      weight,
      opacity,
      fill:false,
      lineCap:'round',
      lineJoin:'round'
    });
  });

  const metricLabel = getMetricLabel(section, metric);
  document.getElementById(`legend-title-${section}`).textContent = `${metricLabel} · ${territory === 'Todos' ? 'Fortaleza' : territory}`;
  document.getElementById(`legend-min-${section}`).textContent = values.length && (section !== 'osc' || hasPositiveOscValues) ? fmt(Math.min(...values), METRICS[metric].fmt) : 'Sem dado';
  document.getElementById(`legend-max-${section}`).textContent = values.length && (section !== 'osc' || hasPositiveOscValues) ? fmt(Math.max(...values), METRICS[metric].fmt) : 'Sem dado';

  if(values.length && (section !== 'osc' || hasPositiveOscValues)){
    const sorted = [...rows].filter(d => Number.isFinite(d[metric])).sort((a,b) => b[metric]-a[metric]);
    const top = sorted[0];
    const bottom = sorted[sorted.length-1];
    const coverage = bairro !== 'Todos'
      ? 'Os demais bairros permanecem em cinza para preservar a leitura integral da malha urbana e manter o contraste visual do recorte selecionado.'
      : 'Os bairros com base analítica aparecem em gradiente vermelho e o restante da cidade permanece em cinza como referência espacial da malha urbana.';
    let extraNote = '';
    if(section === 'osc' && oscPointStats){
      const oscFilterSummary = getOscFilterSummary(oscFilters);
      const thematicFilter = oscFilterSummary.hasSubareaFilter
        ? ` Filtro temático atual: <strong>${oscFilterSummary.subareaLabel}</strong>.`
        : oscFilterSummary.hasAreaFilter
          ? ` Filtro temático atual: <strong>${oscFilterSummary.areaLabel}</strong>.`
          : '';
      extraNote = ` Há <strong>${fmt(oscPointStats.count, 'int')}</strong> OSC(s) ativas geolocalizadas no recorte atual, exibidas como pontos individuais.${thematicFilter}`;
      if(oscPointStats.shifted){
        extraNote += ' Pontos com coordenadas coincidentes foram levemente afastados apenas para facilitar o clique.';
      }
    }
    document.getElementById(`note-${section}`).innerHTML = `<strong>${metricLabel}.</strong> Maior valor em <strong>${top.b}</strong> (${fmt(top[metric], METRICS[metric].fmt)}) e menor valor em <strong>${bottom.b}</strong> (${fmt(bottom[metric], METRICS[metric].fmt)}). ${coverage}${extraNote}`;
  } else {
    document.getElementById(`note-${section}`).textContent = section === 'osc'
      ? 'Não há OSCs ativas geolocalizadas no recorte territorial e temático atual.'
      : 'Não há dados disponíveis para a combinação atual de filtros.';
  }

  renderMapAnalysis(section, territory, bairro, metric, section === 'osc' && !hasPositiveOscValues ? [] : rows);

  setTimeout(() => {
    state.map.invalidateSize();
    if(section === 'osc' && state.oscPointLayer && state.oscPointLayer.getLayers().length){
      const pointBounds = state.oscPointLayer.getBounds();
      if(pointBounds && pointBounds.isValid()){
        state.map.fitBounds(pointBounds.pad(0.08), {padding:[18,18]});
        return;
      }
    }
    if(territory === 'Todos' && bairro === 'Todos'){
      state.map.fitBounds(state.bounds, {padding:[20,20]});
    } else if(focusLayers.length){
      const group = L.featureGroup(focusLayers);
      state.map.fitBounds(group.getBounds(), {padding:[18,18]});
    } else {
      state.map.fitBounds(state.bounds, {padding:[20,20]});
    }
  }, 60);
}

function baseChartOptions() {
  return {
    responsive:true,
    maintainAspectRatio:false,
    layout:{padding:{top:6,right:6,bottom:0,left:0}},
    plugins:{
      legend:{
        labels:{
          font:{family:'Inter', size:11, weight:'600'},
          color:UI_COLORS.muted,
          usePointStyle:true,
          pointStyle:'circle',
          boxWidth:8,
          boxHeight:8,
          padding:16
        }
      },
      tooltip:{
        backgroundColor:UI_COLORS.text,
        titleFont:{family:'Inter', size:12, weight:'700'},
        bodyFont:{family:'Inter', size:12},
        padding:12,
        cornerRadius:12,
        usePointStyle:true,
        displayColors:true
      }
    },
    scales:{
      x:{
        ticks:{color:UI_COLORS.muted, font:{family:'Inter', size:10}},
        grid:{color:'#edf2f6', drawTicks:false},
        border:{display:false}
      },
      y:{
        ticks:{color:UI_COLORS.muted, font:{family:'Inter', size:10, weight:'600'}},
        grid:{display:false, drawTicks:false},
        border:{display:false}
      }
    }
  };
}
function destroyChart(id){
  if(CHARTS[id]){CHARTS[id].destroy();delete CHARTS[id];}
}
function makeBar(id, labels, datasets, extra={}){
  destroyChart(id);
  const canvas = document.getElementById(id);
  if(!canvas) return;
  const barH = Math.max(labels.length*28, 300);
  canvas.style.height = barH+'px';
  canvas.parentElement.style.height = 'auto';
  const opts = baseChartOptions();
  opts.indexAxis = 'y';
  if(extra?.plugins) opts.plugins = {...opts.plugins, ...extra.plugins};
  if(extra?.scales) opts.scales = {...opts.scales, ...extra.scales};
  CHARTS[id] = new Chart(canvas, {
    type:'bar',
    data:{labels, datasets},
    options: opts
  });
}

function addTerritoryLegend(chartId){
  const canvas = document.getElementById(chartId);
  if(!canvas) return;
  const chartBox = canvas.closest('.chart-box');
  if(!chartBox || !chartBox.parentElement) return;
  const panel = chartBox.parentElement;
  const existing = panel.querySelector('[data-territory-legend]');
  if(existing) existing.remove();
  const el = document.createElement('div');
  el.setAttribute('data-territory-legend','true');
  el.style.cssText = 'display:flex;gap:16px;padding:0 20px 8px;font-size:12px;color:#5C6E7E';
  el.innerHTML = `
    <span style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:5px">
      <span style="width:10px;height:10px;background:${UI_COLORS.blueSoft};border-radius:2px;display:inline-block"></span>Grande Mucuripe
    </span>
    <span style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:5px">
      <span style="width:10px;height:10px;background:${UI_COLORS.teal};border-radius:2px;display:inline-block"></span>Grande Bom Jardim
    </span>`;
  panel.insertBefore(el, chartBox);
}

function sortRows(key, descending=true, includeNull=false){
  return [...DATA].filter(d => includeNull ? true : Number.isFinite(d[key])).sort((a,b)=>descending?(b[key]-a[key]):(a[key]-b[key]));
}

const TERR_DESC = {
  'Grande Mucuripe': 'Maior heterogeneidade interna, melhor base econômica média e densidade institucional mais alta. Pede estratégias focalizadas por bairro, ligando bolsões de vulnerabilidade às oportunidades existentes no entorno.',
  'Grande Bom Jardim': 'Maior escala populacional, pobreza mais contínua e ligação direta com os eixos de inclusão produtiva e segurança alimentar. Pede atuação em escala, com foco em adicionalidade social.'
};

function buildTerrCard(name, rows){
  const pop = rows.reduce((s,d)=>s+(d.pop||0),0);
  const emp = rows.reduce((s,d)=>s+(d.emp||0),0);
  const osc = rows.reduce((s,d)=>s+(d.osc||0),0);
  return `<h4>${name}</h4>
    <div class="mini">
      <div><strong>${fmt(pop,'int')}</strong><span class="small">habitantes</span></div>
      <div><strong>${fmt(emp,'int')}</strong><span class="small">empresas</span></div>
      <div><strong>${fmt(osc,'int')}</strong><span class="small">OSCs</span></div>
    </div>`;
}

function refreshOverviewCharts(territory, bairro){
  const rows = filteredData(territory, bairro);
  if(!rows.length) return;

  // KPIs
  const pop = rows.reduce((s,d)=>s+(d.pop||0),0);
  const emp = rows.reduce((s,d)=>s+(d.emp||0),0);
  const osc = rows.reduce((s,d)=>s+(d.osc||0),0);
  const totalPop = rows.reduce((s,d)=>s+(d.pop||0),0);
  const renda = totalPop ? rows.reduce((s,d)=>s+(d.renda||0)*(d.pop||0),0)/totalPop : 0;
  const rendaPC = totalPop ? rows.reduce((s,d)=>s+(d.rendaPC||0)*(d.pop||0),0)/totalPop : 0;
  const suffix = bairro !== 'Todos' ? bairro : territory !== 'Todos' ? territory : `${rows.length} bairros`;
  document.getElementById('overview-kpis').innerHTML = [
    {label:'População total', value:fmt(pop,'int'), sub:`Censo 2022 · ${suffix}`},
    {label:'Empresas ativas', value:fmt(emp,'int'), sub:'Receita Federal · dez/2024'},
    {label:'Rendimento resp. per capita', value:fmt(rendaPC,'money'), sub:'IBGE Censo, 2022'},
    {label:'Renda do responsável', value:fmt(renda,'money'), sub:'IBGE Censo, 2022'}
  ].map(k=>`<div class="kpi"><div class="label">${k.label}</div><div class="value">${k.value}</div><div class="sub">${k.sub}</div></div>`).join('');

  // Territory cards — update content and visibility
  const showBoth = territory === 'Todos' && bairro === 'Todos';
  const showGm = showBoth || territory === 'Grande Mucuripe';
  const showGbj = showBoth || territory === 'Grande Bom Jardim';

  const cardGm = document.getElementById('card-gm');
  const cardGbj = document.getElementById('card-gbj');
  const terrGrid = cardGm.parentElement;
  if(showGm){
    const gmRows = rows.filter(d=>d.t==='Grande Mucuripe');
    if(gmRows.length) cardGm.innerHTML = buildTerrCard('Grande Mucuripe', gmRows);
    cardGm.style.display = '';
  } else {
    cardGm.style.display = 'none';
  }
  if(showGbj){
    const gbjRows = rows.filter(d=>d.t==='Grande Bom Jardim');
    if(gbjRows.length) cardGbj.innerHTML = buildTerrCard('Grande Bom Jardim', gbjRows);
    cardGbj.style.display = '';
  } else {
    cardGbj.style.display = 'none';
  }
  terrGrid.style.display = (showGm || showGbj) ? '' : 'none';

  // Charts
  const rowsEpob = [...rows].sort((a,b)=>(b.epob||0)-(a.epob||0));
  const chartEpob = CHARTS['chart-visao-epob'];
  if(chartEpob){
    chartEpob.data.labels = rowsEpob.map(d=>d.b);
    chartEpob.data.datasets[0].data = rowsEpob.map(d=>d.epob*100);
    chartEpob.data.datasets[0].backgroundColor = rowsEpob.map(d=>d.t==='Grande Mucuripe'?UI_COLORS.blueSoft:UI_COLORS.teal);
    chartEpob.update('none');
  }
  const rowsRendaPC = [...rows].sort((a,b)=>(b.rendaPC||0)-(a.rendaPC||0));
  const chartRendaPC = CHARTS['chart-visao-rendapc'];
  if(chartRendaPC){
    chartRendaPC.data.labels = rowsRendaPC.map(d=>d.b);
    chartRendaPC.data.datasets[0].data = rowsRendaPC.map(d=>Math.round(d.rendaPC||0));
    chartRendaPC.data.datasets[0].backgroundColor = rowsRendaPC.map(d=>d.t==='Grande Mucuripe'?UI_COLORS.blueSoft:UI_COLORS.teal);
    chartRendaPC.update('none');
  }
}

function initOverview(){
  createMap('visao');

  const rowsEpob = sortRows('epob', true);
  makeBar('chart-visao-epob', rowsEpob.map(d=>d.b), [{data:rowsEpob.map(d=>d.epob*100), backgroundColor:rowsEpob.map(d=>d.t==='Grande Mucuripe'?UI_COLORS.blueSoft:UI_COLORS.teal), borderRadius:6}], {
    plugins:{legend:{display:false}}
  });
  addTerritoryLegend('chart-visao-epob');
  const rowsRendaPC = sortRows('rendaPC', true);
  makeBar('chart-visao-rendapc', rowsRendaPC.map(d=>d.b), [{data:rowsRendaPC.map(d=>Math.round(d.rendaPC||0)), backgroundColor:rowsRendaPC.map(d=>d.t==='Grande Mucuripe'?UI_COLORS.blueSoft:UI_COLORS.teal), borderRadius:6}], {
    plugins:{legend:{display:false},tooltip:{callbacks:{label:ctx=>'R$ '+Number(ctx.parsed.x).toLocaleString('pt-BR')}}}
  });
  addTerritoryLegend('chart-visao-rendapc');

  refreshOverviewCharts('Todos', 'Todos');
}

  addTerritoryLegend('chart-demo-dens');
}

  createMap('vuln');
  const epRows = sortRows('epob', true);
  makeBar('chart-vuln-epob', epRows.map(d=>d.b), [{data:epRows.map(d=>d.epob*100), backgroundColor:epRows.map(d=>d.t==='Grande Mucuripe'?UI_COLORS.blueSoft:UI_COLORS.teal), borderRadius:6}], {plugins:{legend:{display:false}}});
  addTerritoryLegend('chart-vuln-epob');
  const idRows = sortRows('idhm', false);
  makeBar('chart-vuln-idhm', idRows.map(d=>d.b), [{data:idRows.map(d=>d.idhm), backgroundColor:idRows.map(d=>d.t==='Grande Mucuripe'?UI_COLORS.blueSoft:UI_COLORS.teal), borderRadius:6}], {
    plugins:{legend:{display:false}},
    scales:{x:{min:0, max:1}}
  });
  addTerritoryLegend('chart-vuln-idhm');
  // Rendimento do responsável per capita (Censo 2022)
  const pcRows=[...DATA].filter(d=>d.rendaPC!=null).sort((a,b)=>a.rendaPC-b.rendaPC);
  makeBar('chart-vuln-rendapc',pcRows.map(d=>d.b),[{data:pcRows.map(d=>Math.round(d.rendaPC)),backgroundColor:pcRows.map(d=>d.t==='Grande Mucuripe'?UI_COLORS.blueSoft:UI_COLORS.teal),borderRadius:6}],{plugins:{legend:{display:false},tooltip:{callbacks:{label:ctx=>'R$ '+Number(ctx.parsed.x).toLocaleString('pt-BR')}}}});
  addTerritoryLegend('chart-vuln-rendapc');
  // Rendimento médio do responsável (Censo 2022)
  const rdRows=[...DATA].sort((a,b)=>(a.renda||0)-(b.renda||0));
  makeBar('chart-vuln-renda',rdRows.map(d=>d.b),[{data:rdRows.map(d=>Math.round(d.renda||0)),backgroundColor:rdRows.map(d=>d.t==='Grande Mucuripe'?UI_COLORS.blueSoft:UI_COLORS.teal),borderRadius:6}],{plugins:{legend:{display:false},tooltip:{callbacks:{label:ctx=>'R$ '+Number(ctx.parsed.x).toLocaleString('pt-BR')}}}});
  addTerritoryLegend('chart-vuln-renda');
}

function initInfra(){
  createMap('infra');
  const rows = sortRows('esg', true);
  destroyChart('chart-infra-san');
  const sanCanvas=document.getElementById('chart-infra-san');
  const sanH=Math.max(rows.length*50,300);
  sanCanvas.style.height=sanH+'px';
  sanCanvas.parentElement.style.height='auto';
  const sanOpts=baseChartOptions();
  sanOpts.indexAxis='y';
  CHARTS['chart-infra-san'] = new Chart(sanCanvas, {
    type:'bar',
    data:{
      labels:rows.map(d=>d.b),
      datasets:[
        {label:'Esgoto', data:rows.map(d=>d.esg*100), backgroundColor:UI_COLORS.blue, borderRadius:5},
        {label:'Água', data:rows.map(d=>d.agua*100), backgroundColor:UI_COLORS.blueSoft, borderRadius:5},
        {label:'Coleta de lixo', data:rows.map(d=>d.lixo*100), backgroundColor:'#d7e5ef', borderRadius:5}
      ]
    },
    options:sanOpts
  });

  const esfRows = [...DATA].filter(d=>Number.isFinite(d.esfc)).sort((a,b)=>b.esfc-a.esfc);
  makeBar('chart-infra-esf', esfRows.map(d=>d.b), [{data:esfRows.map(d=>d.esfc), backgroundColor:esfRows.map(d=>d.esfc<60?UI_COLORS.red:d.esfc<80?UI_COLORS.gold:UI_COLORS.teal), borderRadius:6}], {plugins:{legend:{display:false}}});
  initFavelasMap('fortaleza', GEOJSON);
}


function fmtCap(v){
  if(v>=1e12) return 'R$ '+(v/1e12).toFixed(1)+' tri';
  if(v>=1e9) return 'R$ '+(v/1e9).toFixed(1)+' bi';
  if(v>=1e6) return 'R$ '+(v/1e6).toFixed(1)+' M';
  if(v>=1e3) return 'R$ '+(v/1e3).toFixed(0)+' mil';
  return 'R$ '+v.toFixed(0);
}
function mgeColor(cap){
  if(cap>=1e9) return '#1E3A5F';
  if(cap>=1e8) return '#1E40AF';
  if(cap>=1e7) return '#2563EB';
  if(cap>=1e6) return '#3B82F6';
  if(cap>=1e5) return '#60A5FA';
  return '#93C5FD';
}

function initEcon(){

  // --- Economia: KPIs resumo geral ---
  const totalEmpAll=DATA.reduce((s,d)=>s+(+d.emp||0),0);
  const totalMEI=DATA.reduce((s,d)=>s+(+d.mei||0),0);
  const totalCoops=DATA.reduce((s,d)=>s+(+d.coops||0),0);
  const avgMeiAll=DATA.filter(d=>d.meiShare!=null).reduce((s,d,_,a)=>s+d.meiShare/a.length,0);
  const econResumo=document.getElementById('kpi-econ-resumo');
  if(econResumo) econResumo.innerHTML=`
    <div class="kpi"><div class="label">Total de empresas</div><div class="value">${totalEmpAll.toLocaleString('pt-BR')}</div><div class="sub">Todos os portes · Receita Federal, 2024</div></div>
    <div class="kpi"><div class="label">MEIs</div><div class="value">${totalMEI.toLocaleString('pt-BR')}</div><div class="sub">${(avgMeiAll*100).toFixed(0)}% do total em média</div></div>
    <div class="kpi"><div class="label">Micro e pequenas</div><div class="value">${DATA.reduce((s,d)=>s+(+d.mpe||0),0).toLocaleString('pt-BR')}</div><div class="sub">Porte MPE</div></div>
    <div class="kpi"><div class="label">Cooperativas</div><div class="value">${totalCoops.toLocaleString('pt-BR')}</div><div class="sub">Cooperativas ativas</div></div>`;

  // --- MGE KPIs ---
  const totalMGE=MGE_BUBBLES.length;
  const totalCap=MGE_BAIRRO.reduce((s,b)=>s+b.cap,0);
  const topBairro=MGE_BAIRRO[0];
  const topSetor=MGE_SETOR[0];
  const avgMeiPct=DATA.filter(d=>d.meiShare!=null).reduce((s,d,_,a)=>s+d.meiShare/a.length,0);
  const matrizes=MGE_BUBBLES.filter(r=>r[6]==='M').length;
  document.getElementById('kpi-mge').innerHTML=`
    <div class="kpi"><div class="label">Empresas M/G</div><div class="value">${totalMGE.toLocaleString('pt-BR')}</div><div class="sub">Receita Federal · dez/2024</div></div>
    <div class="kpi"><div class="label">Capital total</div><div class="value">${fmtCap(totalCap)}</div><div class="sub">Capital social acumulado</div></div>
    <div class="kpi"><div class="label">Maior concentração</div><div class="value">${topBairro.b}</div><div class="sub">${topBairro.n} empresas · ${fmtCap(topBairro.cap)}</div></div>
    <div class="kpi"><div class="label">Principal setor</div><div class="value">${topSetor.s}</div><div class="sub">${fmtCap(topSetor.cap)} em capital</div></div>
    <div class="kpi"><div class="label">MEI médio</div><div class="value">${(avgMeiPct*100).toFixed(0)}%</div><div class="sub">Participação média nos bairros</div></div>
    <div class="kpi"><div class="label">Matrizes</div><div class="value">${matrizes.toLocaleString('pt-BR')}</div><div class="sub">${(matrizes/totalMGE*100).toFixed(0)}% são sede · ${(totalMGE-matrizes).toLocaleString('pt-BR')} filiais</div></div>`;

  // --- MGE Charts ---
  const setorTop15=MGE_SETOR.slice(0,15);
  makeBar('chart-mge-setor',setorTop15.map(s=>s.s),[{data:setorTop15.map(s=>s.cap),backgroundColor:UI_COLORS.blue,borderRadius:5}],{plugins:{legend:{display:false},tooltip:{callbacks:{label:ctx=>fmtCap(ctx.parsed.x)}}}});
  makeBar('chart-mge-bairro',MGE_BAIRRO.map(b=>b.b),[{data:MGE_BAIRRO.map(b=>b.n),backgroundColor:UI_COLORS.blue,borderRadius:5}],{plugins:{legend:{display:false}}});

  // --- Populate MGE filters ---
  const selBairroMge=document.getElementById('mge-bairro-filter');
  const selSetorMge=document.getElementById('mge-setor-filter');
  const selTerrMge=document.getElementById('mge-terr-filter');
  const selTipoMge=document.getElementById('mge-tipo-filter');
  const mgeMapSearch=document.getElementById('mge-map-search');
  const bairrosBubble=[...new Set(MGE_BUBBLES.map(r=>r[4]))].sort((a,b)=>a.localeCompare(b,'pt-BR'));
  bairrosBubble.forEach(b=>{const o=document.createElement('option');o.value=b;o.textContent=b;selBairroMge.appendChild(o)});
  const setores=[...new Set(MGE_BUBBLES.map(r=>r[3]))].sort();
  setores.forEach(s=>{const o=document.createElement('option');o.value=s;o.textContent=s;selSetorMge.appendChild(o)});
  ['Grande Mucuripe','Grande Bom Jardim'].forEach(t=>{const o=document.createElement('option');o.value=t;o.textContent=t;selTerrMge.appendChild(o)});

  // --- MGE Map (choropleth + clustering) ---
  const capByBairro={};
  MGE_BAIRRO.forEach(b=>{capByBairro[b.b]=b.cap});
  const maxBairroCap=Math.max(...MGE_BAIRRO.map(b=>b.cap));
  function choroplethColor(cap){
    if(!cap||cap<=0) return '#F5F3FF';
    const t=Math.log10(cap+1)/Math.log10(maxBairroCap+1);
    const stops=['#EFF6FF','#DBEAFE','#BFDBFE','#93C5FD','#60A5FA','#3B82F6','#2563EB','#1D4ED8','#1E40AF'];
    const idx=Math.min(Math.floor(t*stops.length),stops.length-1);
    return stops[idx];
  }
  if(!MAPS['map-mge']){
    MAPS['map-mge']={};
    const m=L.map('map-mge',{zoomControl:false,scrollWheelZoom:true});
    L.tileLayer('https://{s}.basemaps.cartocdn.com/light_all/{z}/{x}/{y}{r}.png',{attribution:'© OSM · CARTO',maxZoom:18}).addTo(m);
    L.control.zoom({position:'bottomleft'}).addTo(m);
    MAPS['map-mge'].map=m;
    MAPS['map-mge'].geo=L.geoJSON(GEOJSON,{
      style:function(f){
        const cap=capByBairro[f.properties.nome]||0;
        return {fillColor:choroplethColor(cap),fillOpacity:0.55,color:'#0B4FA8',weight:1,opacity:0.4};
      },
      onEachFeature:function(f,layer){
        const d=LOOKUP[canonical(f.properties.nome)]||null;
        const mge=MGE_BAIRRO.find(b=>b.b===f.properties.nome);
        const terr=d?d.t:'—';
        const terrCol=terr==='Grande Mucuripe'?UI_COLORS.territoryGM:UI_COLORS.territoryGBJ;
        if(d||mge){
          const capStr=mge?fmtCap(mge.cap):'—';
          const nStr=mge?mge.n:0;
          const avgStr=mge?fmtCap(mge.avg):'—';
          layer.bindPopup(`<div class="mge-popup"><h4>${f.properties.nome}</h4><div style="font-size:10px;font-weight:700;color:${terrCol};margin-bottom:6px">${terr}</div><div class="mge-cap">${capStr}</div><div class="mge-meta">${nStr} empresas M/G · capital médio ${avgStr}<br>Pop. ${d?fmt(d.pop,'int'):'—'} · MEI ${d&&d.meiShare!=null?fmt(d.meiShare,'pct'):'—'}</div></div>`,{maxWidth:300});
        }
        layer.on('mouseover',function(){this.setStyle({weight:2.5,fillOpacity:.7})});
        layer.on('mouseout',function(){MAPS['map-mge'].geo.resetStyle(this)});
      }
    }).addTo(m);
    MAPS['map-mge'].clusterLayer=L.markerClusterGroup({
      maxClusterRadius:45,
      spiderfyOnMaxZoom:true,
      showCoverageOnHover:false,
      iconCreateFunction:function(cluster){
        const count=cluster.getChildCount();
        let cls='mge-cluster-sm';
        if(count>=50) cls='mge-cluster-lg';
        else if(count>=15) cls='mge-cluster-md';
        return L.divIcon({html:'<div class="mge-cluster '+cls+'">'+count+'</div>',className:'',iconSize:[52,52],iconAnchor:[26,26]});
      }
    }).addTo(m);
    m.fitBounds(MAPS['map-mge'].geo.getBounds(),{padding:[20,20]});
  }

  let mgeListVisible=20;
  function getFilteredMGE(){
    const bf=selBairroMge.value;
    const sf=selSetorMge.value;
    const tf=selTerrMge.value;
    const tp=selTipoMge.value;
    const sq=(mgeMapSearch.value||'').toLowerCase();
    let filtered=[...MGE_BUBBLES];
    if(bf) filtered=filtered.filter(r=>r[4]===bf);
    if(sf) filtered=filtered.filter(r=>r[3]===sf);
    if(tf) filtered=filtered.filter(r=>(MGE_TERRITORY_MAP[r[4]]||'')===tf);
    if(tp) filtered=filtered.filter(r=>r[6]===tp);
    if(sq) filtered=filtered.filter(r=>r[5].toLowerCase().includes(sq));
    return filtered.sort((a,b)=>b[2]-a[2]);
  }

  function renderMGEMap(){
    const cl=MAPS['map-mge'].clusterLayer;
    cl.clearLayers();
    const filtered=getFilteredMGE();
    filtered.forEach(r=>{
      const [lat,lng,cap,setor,bairro,nome,tipo,cnpj,razao,endereco,cep,dtIni,cnae,nj]=r;
      const terr=MGE_TERRITORY_MAP[bairro]||'—';
      const terrCol=terr==='Grande Mucuripe'?UI_COLORS.territoryGM||'#0B4FA8':UI_COLORS.territoryGBJ||'#059669';
      const dotColor=mgeColor(cap);
      const mk=L.marker([lat,lng],{
        icon:L.divIcon({
          className:'',
          html:`<div style="width:10px;height:10px;border-radius:50%;background:${dotColor};border:1.5px solid #fff;box-shadow:0 1px 4px rgba(0,0,0,.25)"></div>`,
          iconSize:[10,10],iconAnchor:[5,5]
        })
      });
      const detailRows=[
        cnpj?`<div class="row"><b>CNPJ</b>${cnpj}</div>`:'',
        endereco?`<div class="row"><b>Endereço</b>${endereco}${cep?` · CEP ${cep}`:''}</div>`:''
      ].filter(Boolean).join('');
      mk.bindPopup(`<div class="mge-popup" style="min-width:280px;max-width:340px">
        <h4>${nome}</h4>
        <div style="font-size:10px;font-weight:700;color:${terrCol};margin-bottom:6px">${terr} · ${bairro}</div>
        <div class="mge-cap">${fmtCap(cap)}</div>
        <div style="margin-top:6px;display:flex;gap:4px;flex-wrap:wrap">
          <span class="mge-tag">${setor}</span>
        </div>
        ${detailRows?`<div class="mge-detail">${detailRows}</div>`:''}
      </div>`,{maxWidth:360});
      cl.addLayer(mk);
    });
    mgeListVisible=20;
    renderMGEList();
  }

  function renderMGEList(){
    const filtered=getFilteredMGE();
    const totalCapF=filtered.reduce((s,r)=>s+r[2],0);
    const bf=selBairroMge.value,sf=selSetorMge.value,tf=selTerrMge.value,tp=selTipoMge.value;
    const activeFilters=[bf&&`bairro: ${bf}`,sf&&`setor: ${sf}`,tf&&`território: ${tf}`,tp&&(tp==='M'?'Matrizes':'Filiais')].filter(Boolean);
    document.getElementById('mge-analysis-text').innerHTML=`<strong>${filtered.length.toLocaleString('pt-BR')}</strong> empresa${filtered.length!==1?'s':''} M/G${activeFilters.length?` <span style="color:var(--brand)">(${activeFilters.join(' · ')})</span>`:''} · capital total <strong>${fmtCap(totalCapF)}</strong>`;
    const shown=filtered.slice(0,mgeListVisible);
    const listEl=document.getElementById('mge-top-list');
    listEl.innerHTML=shown.map((r,i)=>{
      const terr=MGE_TERRITORY_MAP[r[4]]||'';
      const terrCol=terr==='Grande Mucuripe'?UI_COLORS.territoryGM||'#0B4FA8':UI_COLORS.territoryGBJ||'#059669';
      return `<div style="padding:5px 0;border-bottom:1px solid var(--line-2)">
        <div style="display:flex;gap:6px;align-items:center">
          <span style="font-weight:600;color:var(--muted);font-size:10px;min-width:18px">${i+1}</span>
          <span style="flex:1;font-size:11px;font-weight:500;color:var(--navy);line-height:1.3">${r[5]}</span>
          <span style="font-weight:500;color:#0B4FA8;font-size:11px;white-space:nowrap">${fmtCap(r[2])}</span>
        </div>
        <div style="padding-left:24px;margin-top:2px;display:flex;gap:4px;flex-wrap:wrap;align-items:center">
          <span style="width:6px;height:6px;border-radius:50%;background:${terrCol};display:inline-block;flex-shrink:0"></span>
          <span style="font-size:10px;color:var(--muted)">${r[4]}</span>
          <span style="font-size:10px;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:3px;background:#EFF6FF;color:#0B4FA8;font-weight:600">${r[3]}</span>
          <span style="font-size:10px;font-weight:600;color:${r[6]==='M'?'#0B4FA8':'#7c5c00'}">${r[6]==='M'?'Matriz':'Filial'}</span>
        </div>
      </div>`;
    }).join('')+(mgeListVisible<filtered.length?`<div style="text-align:center;padding:10px;font-size:11px;color:var(--muted)">Exibindo ${mgeListVisible} de ${filtered.length} — role para ver mais ↓</div>`:'');
  }
  document.getElementById('mge-top-list').addEventListener('scroll',function(){
    if(this.scrollTop+this.clientHeight>=this.scrollHeight-30){
      const total=getFilteredMGE().length;
      if(mgeListVisible<total){
        mgeListVisible=Math.min(mgeListVisible+20,total);
        renderMGEList();
      }
    }
  });

  function updateFilterUI(){
    const bf=selBairroMge.value,sf=selSetorMge.value,tf=selTerrMge.value,tp=selTipoMge.value,sq=mgeMapSearch.value;
    const active=[bf,sf,tf,tp,sq].filter(Boolean).length;
    const clearBtn=document.getElementById('mge-clear-filters');
    const badge=document.getElementById('mge-filter-count');
    clearBtn.style.display=active?'':'none';
    badge.style.display=active?'':'none';
    if(active) badge.textContent=`${active} filtro${active>1?'s':''}`;
    selBairroMge.classList.toggle('active-filter',!!bf);
    selSetorMge.classList.toggle('active-filter',!!sf);
    selTerrMge.classList.toggle('active-filter',!!tf);
    selTipoMge.classList.toggle('active-filter',!!tp);
    mgeMapSearch.classList.toggle('active-filter',!!sq);
    renderMGEMap();
  }
  renderMGEMap();
  selBairroMge.addEventListener('change',updateFilterUI);
  selSetorMge.addEventListener('change',updateFilterUI);
  selTerrMge.addEventListener('change',updateFilterUI);
  selTipoMge.addEventListener('change',updateFilterUI);
  mgeMapSearch.addEventListener('input',updateFilterUI);
  document.getElementById('mge-clear-filters').addEventListener('click',()=>{
    selBairroMge.value='';selSetorMge.value='';selTerrMge.value='';selTipoMge.value='';mgeMapSearch.value='';
    updateFilterUI();
  });

  // --- Original econ charts ---

  const empRows = sortRows('emp', true);
  makeBar('chart-econ-emp', empRows.map(d=>d.b), [{data:empRows.map(d=>d.emp), backgroundColor:empRows.map(d=>d.t==='Grande Mucuripe'?UI_COLORS.blueSoft:UI_COLORS.teal), borderRadius:6}], {plugins:{legend:{display:false}}});
  addTerritoryLegend('chart-econ-emp');
  const meiRows = sortRows('meiShare', true);
  makeBar('chart-econ-mei', meiRows.map(d=>d.b), [{data:meiRows.map(d=>(d.meiShare||0)*100), backgroundColor:meiRows.map(d=>d.t==='Grande Mucuripe'?UI_COLORS.blueSoft:UI_COLORS.teal), borderRadius:6}], {plugins:{legend:{display:false},tooltip:{callbacks:{label:ctx=>ctx.parsed.x.toFixed(1)+'%'}}}});
  addTerritoryLegend('chart-econ-mei');
  // ── Tabela Maracanaú-style (sortable columns) ──
  const fmtInt = n => (n==null||isNaN(n))?'—':Number(n).toLocaleString('pt-BR');
  const fmtPct = n => (n==null||isNaN(n))?'—':(Number(n)*100).toLocaleString('pt-BR',{minimumFractionDigits:1,maximumFractionDigits:1})+'%';
  const econSearchEl = document.getElementById('econ-table-search');
  const econTerrEl = document.getElementById('econ-table-terr');
  const econCountEl = document.getElementById('econ-table-count');
  let econSortKey='emp';
  let econSortDir=-1;
  function renderCnaeTable(){
    const q = (econSearchEl.value||'').toLowerCase().trim();
    const tf = econTerrEl.value;
    let rows = [...DATA].filter(d=>d.emp!=null);
    if(q) rows = rows.filter(d => d.b.toLowerCase().includes(q));
    if(tf) rows = rows.filter(d => d.t === tf);
    rows.sort((a,b)=>{
      let va,vb;
      if(econSortKey==='terr'){va=(a.t||'');vb=(b.t||'');}
      else{va=a[econSortKey];vb=b[econSortKey];}
      if(va==null) return 1; if(vb==null) return -1;
      if(typeof va==='string') return econSortDir*va.localeCompare(vb,'pt-BR');
      return econSortDir*(va-vb);
    });
    if(econCountEl) econCountEl.textContent = `${rows.length} bairro${rows.length!==1?'s':''}`;
    const colHeaders=[
      {k:'b',label:'Bairro'},{k:'emp',label:'Empresas (2024)'},{k:'mei',label:'MEIs (2024)'},
      {k:'meiShare',label:'% MEI (2024)'},{k:'mpe',label:'Micro/Peq. (2024)'},{k:'mge',label:'Méd./Grd. (2024)'},
      {k:'coops',label:'Coops. (2024)'},{k:'terr',label:'Território'}
    ];
    document.getElementById('table-econ').innerHTML = `<thead><tr>
      ${colHeaders.map(h=>`<th class="sortable${econSortKey===h.k?' sort-'+(econSortDir===1?'asc':'desc'):''}" data-econ-col="${h.k}">${h.label}<span class="sort-icon">${econSortKey===h.k?(econSortDir===1?'↑':'↓'):'↕'}</span></th>`).join('')}
      <th>Principal CNAE</th>
    </tr></thead><tbody>${rows.map(d=>{
      const terrCol=d.t==='Grande Mucuripe'?UI_COLORS.territoryGM||'#0B4FA8':UI_COLORS.territoryGBJ||'#059669';
      const meiHigh=d.meiShare!=null&&d.meiShare>.8;
      const meiMed=d.meiShare!=null&&d.meiShare>.6&&d.meiShare<=.8;
      return `<tr${meiHigh?' class="mei-high"':''}>
        <td>${d.b}</td>
        <td>${fmtInt(d.emp)}</td>
        <td>${fmtInt(d.mei)}</td>
        <td style="color:${meiHigh?'#B07C20':meiMed?'#0B4FA8':'var(--text)'};font-weight:500">${d.meiShare!=null?fmtPct(d.meiShare):'—'}</td>
        <td>${fmtInt(d.mpe)}</td>
        <td>${fmtInt(d.mge)}</td>
        <td>${fmtInt(d.coops)}</td>
        <td><span style="display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:5px;font-size:11px;white-space:nowrap"><span style="width:8px;height:8px;border-radius:50%;background:${terrCol};flex-shrink:0"></span>${d.t||'—'}</span></td>
        <td style="font-size:11px;max-width:200px;color:var(--muted)">${d.cn1||'—'}</td>
      </tr>`;
    }).join('')}</tbody>`;
    document.querySelectorAll('#table-econ th.sortable').forEach(th=>{
      th.addEventListener('click',()=>{
        const col=th.dataset.econCol;
        if(econSortKey===col) econSortDir*=-1;
        else{econSortKey=col;econSortDir=-1;}
        renderCnaeTable();
      });
    });
  }
  renderCnaeTable();
  econSearchEl.addEventListener('input', renderCnaeTable);
  econTerrEl.addEventListener('change', renderCnaeTable);

  // ── Lista completa de empresas M/G ──
  let mgeTableSort='cap', mgeTableDir=-1;
  function renderMGETable(){
    const q=(document.getElementById('mge-table-search').value||'').toLowerCase().trim();
    const tf=document.getElementById('mge-table-terr').value;
    const sf=document.getElementById('mge-table-setor').value;
    const tp=document.getElementById('mge-table-tipo').value;
    let rows=[...MGE_BUBBLES];
    if(q) rows=rows.filter(r=>r[5].toLowerCase().includes(q)||r[4].toLowerCase().includes(q)||r[3].toLowerCase().includes(q));
    if(tf) rows=rows.filter(r=>(MGE_TERRITORY_MAP[r[4]]||'')=== tf);
    if(sf) rows=rows.filter(r=>r[3]===sf);
    if(tp) rows=rows.filter(r=>r[6]===tp);
    if(mgeTableSort==='terr'){
      rows.sort((a,b)=>{
        const at=(MGE_TERRITORY_MAP[a[4]]||'').toLowerCase();
        const bt=(MGE_TERRITORY_MAP[b[4]]||'').toLowerCase();
        return mgeTableDir===-1?bt.localeCompare(at,'pt'):at.localeCompare(bt,'pt');
      });
    } else {
      const sortIdx={nome:5,cap:2,setor:3,bairro:4,tipo:6}[mgeTableSort]||2;
      rows.sort((a,b)=>{
        const av=typeof a[sortIdx]==='string'?a[sortIdx].toLowerCase():a[sortIdx];
        const bv=typeof b[sortIdx]==='string'?b[sortIdx].toLowerCase():b[sortIdx];
        if(typeof av==='number'&&typeof bv==='number') return mgeTableDir===-1?bv-av:av-bv;
        return mgeTableDir===-1?String(bv).localeCompare(String(av),'pt'):String(av).localeCompare(String(bv),'pt');
      });
    }
    const countEl=document.getElementById('mge-table-count');
    if(countEl) countEl.textContent=rows.length+' de '+MGE_BUBBLES.length+' empresas';
    const cols=[{k:'nome',label:'Empresa'},{k:'cap',label:'Capital Social'},{k:'setor',label:'Setor'},{k:'bairro',label:'Bairro'},{k:'terr',label:'Território'},{k:'tipo',label:'Tipo'}];
    const tbl=document.getElementById('table-mge-list');
    if(!tbl) return;
    const terrColors={'Grande Mucuripe':'#0B4FA8','Grande Bom Jardim':'#1E40AF'};
    tbl.innerHTML=`<thead><tr>${cols.map(c=>`<th class="sortable${mgeTableSort===c.k?' sort-'+(mgeTableDir===1?'asc':'desc'):''}" data-mge-col="${c.k}">${c.label}<span class="sort-icon">${mgeTableSort===c.k?(mgeTableDir===1?'↑':'↓'):'↕'}</span></th>`).join('')}</tr></thead><tbody>${rows.slice(0,500).map(r=>{
      const terr=MGE_TERRITORY_MAP[r[4]]||'';
      const tCol=terrColors[terr]||'#999';
      return `<tr><td style="font-weight:600;max-width:280px;overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis;white-space:nowrap" title="${r[5]}">${r[5]}</td><td data-sort-value="${r[2]}" style="white-space:nowrap">${fmtCap(r[2])}</td><td><span class="sector-tag">${r[3]}</span></td><td><span class="region-dot" style="background:${tCol};margin-right:4px"></span>${r[4]}</td><td><span style="display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:5px;font-size:11px;white-space:nowrap"><span class="region-dot" style="background:${tCol}"></span>${terr}</span></td><td><span class="tipo-badge ${r[6]==='M'?'tipo-m':'tipo-f'}">${r[6]==='M'?'Matriz':'Filial'}</span></td></tr>`;
    }).join('')}${rows.length>500?`<tr><td colspan="6" style="text-align:center;color:var(--muted);font-size:12px;padding:12px">Mostrando 500 de ${rows.length} empresas. Use os filtros para refinar.</td></tr>`:''}</tbody>`;
    document.querySelectorAll('#table-mge-list th.sortable').forEach(th=>{
      th.onclick=()=>{
        const col=th.dataset.mgeCol;
        if(mgeTableSort===col) mgeTableDir*=-1;
        else{mgeTableSort=col;mgeTableDir=-1;}
        renderMGETable();
      };
    });
  }
  // Populate MGE table filters
  const mgeSetSel=document.getElementById('mge-table-setor');
  if(mgeSetSel&&mgeSetSel.options.length<=1){
    const setores=[...new Set(MGE_BUBBLES.map(r=>r[3]))].sort();
    setores.forEach(s=>{const o=document.createElement('option');o.value=s;o.textContent=s;mgeSetSel.appendChild(o);});
  }
  renderMGETable();
  ['mge-table-search','mge-table-terr','mge-table-setor','mge-table-tipo'].forEach(id=>{
    const el=document.getElementById(id);
    if(el) el.addEventListener(el.tagName==='INPUT'?'input':'change',renderMGETable);
  });

  // --- Mapa do setor alimentício ---
  initAlimentForMap();
}

const ALIMENT_CAT = {
  R: {label:'Restaurantes & Lanchonetes', color:'#DC2626', icon:'🍴'},
  H: {label:'Hortifruti',                  color:'#16A34A', icon:'🥬'},
  A: {label:'Atacado especializado',       color:'#0D9488', icon:'📦'},
  S: {label:'Hiper & Supermercados',       color:'#7C3AED', icon:'🛒'}
};

function alimentMarkerIcon(cat){
  const c = ALIMENT_CAT[cat] || ALIMENT_CAT.R;
  return L.divIcon({
    className:'aliment-marker',
    html:`<div class="am-dot" style="background:${c.color}">${c.icon}</div>`,
    iconSize:[22,22],
    iconAnchor:[11,11]
  });
}

function initAlimentForMap(){
  if(typeof ALIMENT_FOR_POINTS==='undefined' || !document.getElementById('map-aliment')) return;

  // Counts per bairro for choropleth
  const totalByBairro = {};
  ALIMENT_FOR_POINTS.forEach(p=>{ totalByBairro[p.b] = (totalByBairro[p.b]||0) + 1; });
  const maxBairroAliment = Math.max(...Object.values(totalByBairro), 1);

  function alimentChoropleth(n){
    if(!n) return '#EFF6FF';
    const t = Math.log10(n+1)/Math.log10(maxBairroAliment+1);
    const stops = ['#EFF6FF','#DBEAFE','#BFDBFE','#93C5FD','#60A5FA','#3B82F6','#2563EB','#1D4ED8'];
    return stops[Math.min(Math.floor(t*stops.length), stops.length-1)];
  }

  const TERR_COLORS = {'Grande Bom Jardim':'#1E40AF','Grande Mucuripe':'#0B4FA8'};

  // Refs
  const selCat    = document.getElementById('aliment-cat-filter');
  const selTipo   = document.getElementById('aliment-tipo-filter');
  const selTerr   = document.getElementById('aliment-terr-filter');
  const selBairro = document.getElementById('aliment-bairro-filter');
  const inpSearch = document.getElementById('aliment-map-search');
  const btnClear  = document.getElementById('aliment-clear-filters');
  const badge     = document.getElementById('aliment-filter-count');
  const txtAnalysis = document.getElementById('aliment-analysis-text');
  const listEl      = document.getElementById('aliment-top-list');
  const chkTerr     = document.getElementById('aliment-terr-toggle');

  // Populate bairro filter
  const bairros = [...new Set(ALIMENT_FOR_POINTS.map(p=>p.b))].sort((a,b)=>a.localeCompare(b,'pt-BR'));
  bairros.forEach(b=>{
    const opt = document.createElement('option');
    opt.value = b; opt.textContent = `${b} (${totalByBairro[b]})`;
    selBairro.appendChild(opt);
  });

  if(!MAPS['map-aliment']){
    MAPS['map-aliment'] = {};
    const m = L.map('map-aliment',{zoomControl:false, scrollWheelZoom:true});
    L.tileLayer('https://{s}.basemaps.cartocdn.com/light_all/{z}/{x}/{y}{r}.png',{attribution:'© OSM · CARTO', maxZoom:18}).addTo(m);
    L.control.zoom({position:'bottomleft'}).addTo(m);
    MAPS['map-aliment'].map = m;

    // Base choropleth layer (all 121 bairros)
    MAPS['map-aliment'].geo = L.geoJSON(GEOJSON, {
      style: f => ({
        fillColor: alimentChoropleth(totalByBairro[f.properties.nome]||0),
        fillOpacity:0.55, color:'#0B4FA8', weight:1, opacity:0.4
      }),
      onEachFeature: (f, layer) => {
        const n = totalByBairro[f.properties.nome] || 0;
        const terr = f.properties.territorio;
        const head = terr ? `<strong>${f.properties.nome}</strong><br><span style="font-weight:700;color:${TERR_COLORS[terr]||'#666'}">${terr}</span>` : `<strong>${f.properties.nome}</strong>`;
        if(n>0) layer.bindTooltip(`${head}<br>${n} estabelecimento${n>1?'s':''} alimentício${n>1?'s':''}`, {sticky:true});
        layer.on('mouseover', function(){ this.setStyle({weight:2.5, fillOpacity:0.72}); });
        layer.on('mouseout',  function(){ MAPS['map-aliment'].geo.resetStyle(this); });
      }
    }).addTo(m);

    // Two overlay layers — one per território (only outlined, no fill)
    function buildTerrLayer(terrName){
      const feats = GEOJSON.features.filter(f=>f.properties.territorio===terrName);
      return L.geoJSON({type:'FeatureCollection', features:feats}, {
        style: {
          color: TERR_COLORS[terrName], weight:2.8, opacity:0.95,
          fillColor: TERR_COLORS[terrName], fillOpacity:0.08,
          dashArray:'4 3', interactive:false
        }
      });
    }
    MAPS['map-aliment'].terrGBJ = buildTerrLayer('Grande Bom Jardim').addTo(m);
    MAPS['map-aliment'].terrGM  = buildTerrLayer('Grande Mucuripe').addTo(m);

    // Marker cluster
    MAPS['map-aliment'].clusterLayer = L.markerClusterGroup({
      maxClusterRadius: 50,
      spiderfyOnMaxZoom: true,
      showCoverageOnHover: false,
      chunkedLoading: true,
      iconCreateFunction: function(cluster){
        const count = cluster.getChildCount();
        let cls = 'mge-cluster-sm';
        if(count >= 250) cls = 'mge-cluster-lg';
        else if(count >= 60) cls = 'mge-cluster-md';
        return L.divIcon({html:'<div class="mge-cluster '+cls+'">'+count+'</div>', className:'', iconSize:[52,52], iconAnchor:[26,26]});
      }
    }).addTo(m);

    m.fitBounds(MAPS['map-aliment'].geo.getBounds(), {padding:[20,20]});
  }

  function getFilteredAliment(){
    const cf = selCat.value, tf = selTipo.value, trf = selTerr.value, bf = selBairro.value;
    const sq = (inpSearch.value||'').trim().toLowerCase();
    let out = ALIMENT_FOR_POINTS;
    if(cf)  out = out.filter(p=>p.cat===cf);
    if(tf)  out = out.filter(p=>p.tipo===tf);
    if(trf==='__OUTROS__') out = out.filter(p=>!p.t);
    else if(trf) out = out.filter(p=>p.t===trf);
    if(bf)  out = out.filter(p=>p.b===bf);
    if(sq){
      out = out.filter(p=>
        (p.n||'').toLowerCase().includes(sq) ||
        (p.rf||'').toLowerCase().includes(sq) ||
        (p.id||'').includes(sq)
      );
    }
    return out;
  }

  function fmtCnpj(c){
    if(!c || c.length!==14) return c;
    return `${c.slice(0,2)}.${c.slice(2,5)}.${c.slice(5,8)}/${c.slice(8,12)}-${c.slice(12)}`;
  }

  function renderAlimentMap(){
    const cl = MAPS['map-aliment'].clusterLayer;
    cl.clearLayers();
    const filtered = getFilteredAliment();
    const markers = [];
    filtered.forEach(p=>{
      const c = ALIMENT_CAT[p.cat] || ALIMENT_CAT.R;
      const desc = ALIMENT_FOR_CNAE_DESC[p.cnae] || '—';
      const terrTag = p.t ? `<span class="sector-tag" style="background:${TERR_COLORS[p.t]||'#888'};color:#fff">${p.t}</span>` : '';
      const mk = L.marker([p.lat, p.lon], {icon: alimentMarkerIcon(p.cat)});
      mk.bindPopup(`<div class="mge-popup" style="min-width:260px;max-width:340px">
        <h4>${p.n}</h4>
        <div style="font-size:10px;font-weight:700;color:${c.color};margin-bottom:6px;display:flex;align-items:center;gap:6px">
          <span class="aliment-cat-icon" style="background:${c.color};width:14px;height:14px;font-size:8px">${c.icon}</span>${c.label}
        </div>
        <div style="margin-top:6px;display:flex;gap:4px;flex-wrap:wrap">
          <span class="tipo-badge ${p.tipo==='M'?'tipo-m':'tipo-f'}">${p.tipo==='M'?'Matriz':'Filial'}</span>
          <span class="sector-tag">${p.b}</span>
          ${terrTag}
        </div>
        <div class="mge-detail">
          <div class="row"><b>CNPJ</b>${fmtCnpj(p.id)}</div>
          <div class="row"><b>Razão social</b>${p.rf || p.n}</div>
          <div class="row"><b>CNAE</b>${p.cnae} · ${desc}</div>
          ${p.addr?`<div class="row"><b>Endereço cadastral</b>${p.addr}${p.cep?` · CEP ${p.cep}`:''}</div>`:''}
        </div>
        <div style="margin-top:6px;font-size:10px;color:#94a3b8;font-style:italic">Posição aproximada (centroide do bairro com jitter)</div>
      </div>`, {maxWidth:340});
      markers.push(mk);
    });
    cl.addLayers(markers);

    // Side panel
    const total = filtered.length;
    const byCat = {R:0,H:0,A:0,S:0};
    filtered.forEach(p=>{ byCat[p.cat] = (byCat[p.cat]||0) + 1; });
    const matrizes = filtered.filter(p=>p.tipo==='M').length;
    const terrCounts = {GBJ:0, GM:0, OUT:0};
    filtered.forEach(p=>{
      if(p.t==='Grande Bom Jardim') terrCounts.GBJ++;
      else if(p.t==='Grande Mucuripe') terrCounts.GM++;
      else terrCounts.OUT++;
    });

    txtAnalysis.innerHTML = `<strong>${total.toLocaleString('pt-BR')}</strong> estabelecimento${total!==1?'s':''} · ${matrizes} matriz${matrizes!==1?'es':''} e ${total-matrizes} filia${total-matrizes!==1?'is':'l'}`;

    const catRows = ['R','H','A','S'].filter(k=>byCat[k]>0).map(k=>{
      const c = ALIMENT_CAT[k];
