      const pct = total ? ((byCat[k]/total)*100).toFixed(0) : 0;
      return `<div class="aliment-row" style="background:#FAFBFD">
        <span class="aliment-cat-icon" style="background:${c.color}">${c.icon}</span>
        <span class="nm">${c.label}</span>
        <span class="meta"><strong>${byCat[k]}</strong> · ${pct}%</span>
      </div>`;
    }).join('');

    const terrRows = `<div class="aliment-row" style="background:#FAFBFD">
      <span class="terr-dot" style="background:#1E40AF"></span>
      <span class="nm">Grande Bom Jardim</span>
      <span class="meta"><strong>${terrCounts.GBJ}</strong></span>
    </div>
    <div class="aliment-row" style="background:#FAFBFD">
      <span class="terr-dot" style="background:#0B4FA8"></span>
      <span class="nm">Grande Mucuripe</span>
      <span class="meta"><strong>${terrCounts.GM}</strong></span>
    </div>
    <div class="aliment-row" style="background:#FAFBFD">
      <span class="terr-dot" style="background:#9CA3AF"></span>
      <span class="nm">Demais bairros</span>
      <span class="meta"><strong>${terrCounts.OUT}</strong></span>
    </div>`;

    const listVisible = 80;
    const sliced = filtered.slice(0, listVisible);
    const rows = sliced.map(p=>{
      const c = ALIMENT_CAT[p.cat] || ALIMENT_CAT.R;
      const dotStyle = p.t ? `background:${TERR_COLORS[p.t]}` : `background:#9CA3AF`;
      return `<div class="aliment-row" title="${(p.rf||p.n).replace(/"/g,'')}">
        <span class="aliment-cat-icon" style="background:${c.color}">${c.icon}</span>
        <span class="nm">${p.n}</span>
        <span class="meta"><span class="terr-dot" style="${dotStyle};display:inline-block;margin-right:4px"></span>${p.b}</span>
      </div>`;
    }).join('');
    const more = filtered.length > listVisible ? `<div style="padding:8px;text-align:center;font-size:11px;color:var(--muted);font-style:italic">+ ${(filtered.length-listVisible).toLocaleString('pt-BR')} estabelecimentos não exibidos · refine o filtro</div>` : '';

    listEl.innerHTML =
      `<div style="margin-bottom:6px">${catRows}</div>` +
      `<div style="font-size:10px;font-weight:700;color:var(--muted);text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.05em;padding:6px 8px 4px;border-top:1px dashed var(--line-2)">Por território</div>` +
      `<div style="margin-bottom:6px">${terrRows}</div>` +
      `<div style="font-size:10px;font-weight:700;color:var(--muted);text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.05em;padding:6px 8px 4px;border-top:1px dashed var(--line-2)">Lista (${Math.min(listVisible,filtered.length)} de ${filtered.length.toLocaleString('pt-BR')})</div>` +
      rows + more;

    // Filter badge
    const active = (selCat.value?1:0)+(selTipo.value?1:0)+(selTerr.value?1:0)+(selBairro.value?1:0)+(inpSearch.value?1:0);
    badge.style.display = active ? 'inline-block':'none';
    btnClear.style.display = active ? 'inline-flex':'none';
    if(active) badge.textContent = `${active} filtro${active>1?'s':''}`;
    [selCat,selTipo,selTerr,selBairro].forEach(s=>s.classList.toggle('active-filter',!!s.value));
    inpSearch.classList.toggle('active-filter', !!inpSearch.value);
  }

  // Toggle territory layers
  chkTerr.addEventListener('change', ()=>{
    const on = chkTerr.checked;
    const m = MAPS['map-aliment'].map;
    if(on){
      if(!m.hasLayer(MAPS['map-aliment'].terrGBJ)) MAPS['map-aliment'].terrGBJ.addTo(m);
      if(!m.hasLayer(MAPS['map-aliment'].terrGM))  MAPS['map-aliment'].terrGM.addTo(m);
    } else {
      m.removeLayer(MAPS['map-aliment'].terrGBJ);
      m.removeLayer(MAPS['map-aliment'].terrGM);
    }
  });

  selCat.addEventListener('change', renderAlimentMap);
  selTipo.addEventListener('change', renderAlimentMap);
  selTerr.addEventListener('change', renderAlimentMap);
  selBairro.addEventListener('change', renderAlimentMap);
  inpSearch.addEventListener('input', renderAlimentMap);
  btnClear.addEventListener('click', ()=>{
    selCat.value=''; selTipo.value=''; selTerr.value=''; selBairro.value=''; inpSearch.value='';
    renderAlimentMap();
  });

  renderAlimentMap();
  setTimeout(()=>{ try{ MAPS['map-aliment'].map.invalidateSize(); }catch(e){} }, 80);
}


function initOsc(){
  // KPIs
  const totalOsc=OSC_POINTS.length;
  const allAreas=[...new Set(OSC_POINTS.flatMap(p=>p.areas||[]))].filter(a=>a&&a!=='Sem classificação');
  const totalBairros=[...new Set(OSC_POINTS.map(p=>p.b))].length;
  const totalMatriz=OSC_POINTS.filter(p=>p.tipo==='Matriz').length;

  // Top area by count
  const kpiAreaCounts={};
  OSC_POINTS.forEach(p=>{(p.areas||[]).forEach(a=>{kpiAreaCounts[a]=(kpiAreaCounts[a]||0)+1;})});
  const topArea=Object.entries(kpiAreaCounts).filter(([a])=>a!=='Sem classificação').sort((a,b)=>b[1]-a[1])[0];

  // OSCs by territory
  const oscByTerritory={};
  OSC_POINTS.forEach(p=>{const t=p.t||'—';oscByTerritory[t]=(oscByTerritory[t]||0)+1;});

  const kpiEl=document.getElementById('kpi-osc');
  if(kpiEl) kpiEl.innerHTML=[
    {l:'OSCs ativas geolocalizadas',v:fmt(totalOsc,'int'),s:'Base IPEA 2024'},
    {l:'Bairros cobertos',v:fmt(totalBairros,'int'),s:'Com presença de OSCs'},
    {l:'Área predominante',v:topArea?(OSC_AREA_SHORT_LABELS[topArea[0]]||topArea[0]):'—',s:`${topArea?topArea[1]:0} entidades (${topArea?Math.round(topArea[1]/totalOsc*100):0}%)`},
    {l:'Matrizes (sedes)',v:fmt(totalMatriz,'int'),s:`${(totalMatriz/totalOsc*100).toFixed(0)}% do total`},
    {l:'Grande Mucuripe',v:fmt(oscByTerritory['Grande Mucuripe']||0,'int'),s:'OSCs no território'},
    {l:'Grande Bom Jardim',v:fmt(oscByTerritory['Grande Bom Jardim']||0,'int'),s:'OSCs no território'}
  ].map(k=>`<div class="kpi"><div class="label">${k.l}</div><div class="value">${k.v}</div><div class="sub">${k.s}</div></div>`).join('');

  // --- Populate filters ---
  const selBairro=document.getElementById('osc-bairro-filter');
  const selTerr=document.getElementById('osc-territorio-filter');
  const selArea=document.getElementById('osc-area-filter');
  const oscBairros=[...new Set(OSC_POINTS.map(p=>p.b))].sort((a,b)=>a.localeCompare(b,'pt-BR'));
  oscBairros.forEach(b=>{const o=document.createElement('option');o.value=b;o.textContent=b;selBairro.appendChild(o)});
  ['Grande Mucuripe','Grande Bom Jardim'].forEach(t=>{const o=document.createElement('option');o.value=t;o.textContent=t;selTerr.appendChild(o)});
  const oscAreasList=[...new Set(OSC_POINTS.flatMap(p=>p.areas||[]))].filter(a=>a&&a!=='Sem classificação').sort();
  oscAreasList.forEach(a=>{const o=document.createElement('option');o.value=a;o.textContent=OSC_AREA_SHORT_LABELS[a]||a;selArea.appendChild(o)});

  // Build OSC count per bairro for choropleth
  const oscByBairro={};
  OSC_POINTS.forEach(p=>{oscByBairro[p.b]=(oscByBairro[p.b]||0)+1;});
  const maxOscBairro=Math.max(...Object.values(oscByBairro));
  function oscChoroplethColor(n){
    if(!n||n<=0) return '#f3f6fb';
    const t=Math.log10(n+1)/Math.log10(maxOscBairro+1);
    const stops=['#f3f6fb','#dbe6f0','#b9ccdb','#84a8bf','#4f7b98','#1f4d6b','#0B4FA8','#0A3B7A','#082C5C'];
    return stops[Math.min(Math.floor(t*stops.length),stops.length-1)];
  }

  // Map
  if(!MAPS['map-osc']){
    MAPS['map-osc']={};
    const m=L.map('map-osc',{zoomControl:false,scrollWheelZoom:true});
    L.tileLayer('https://{s}.basemaps.cartocdn.com/light_all/{z}/{x}/{y}{r}.png',{attribution:'© OSM · CARTO',maxZoom:18}).addTo(m);
    L.control.zoom({position:'bottomleft'}).addTo(m);
    MAPS['map-osc'].map=m;

    // GeoJSON choropleth by OSC density
    MAPS['map-osc'].geo=L.geoJSON(GEOJSON,{
      style:function(f){
        const n=oscByBairro[f.properties.nome]||0;
        return {fillColor:oscChoroplethColor(n),fillOpacity:0.55,color:'#0B4FA8',weight:1,opacity:0.4};
      },
      onEachFeature:function(f,layer){
        const nome=f.properties.nome;
        const n=oscByBairro[nome]||0;
        const d=LOOKUP[canonical(nome)]||null;
        const terr=d?d.t:'—';
        const terrCol=terr==='Grande Mucuripe'?UI_COLORS.territoryGM:UI_COLORS.territoryGBJ;
        layer.bindPopup(`<div class="mge-popup"><h4>${nome}</h4><div style="font-size:10px;font-weight:700;color:${terrCol};margin-bottom:6px">${terr}</div><div class="mge-cap">${n} OSCs</div><div class="mge-meta">Pop. ${d?fmt(d.pop,'int'):'—'}</div></div>`,{maxWidth:300});
        layer.on('mouseover',function(){this.setStyle({weight:2.5,fillOpacity:.7})});
        layer.on('mouseout',function(){MAPS['map-osc'].geo.resetStyle(this)});
      }
    }).addTo(m);

    // MarkerCluster
    MAPS['map-osc'].clusterLayer=L.markerClusterGroup({
      maxClusterRadius:45,
      spiderfyOnMaxZoom:true,
      showCoverageOnHover:false,
      iconCreateFunction:function(cluster){
        const count=cluster.getChildCount();
        let size=36,bg='#93C5FD',text='#0B4FA8';
        if(count>=50){size=48;bg='#0B4FA8';text='#fff';}
        else if(count>=15){size=42;bg='#4f7b98';text='#fff';}
        return L.divIcon({html:`<div style="width:${size}px;height:${size}px;border-radius:50%;background:${bg};display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;font-size:12px;font-weight:700;color:${text};border:2px solid #fff;box-shadow:0 2px 8px rgba(0,0,0,.25)">${count}</div>`,className:'',iconSize:[size,size],iconAnchor:[size/2,size/2]});
      }
    }).addTo(m);
    const cluster=MAPS['map-osc'].clusterLayer;

    OSC_POINTS.forEach(p=>{
      const primaryArea=p.primary||'Sem classificação';
      const color=OSC_CATEGORY_COLORS[primaryArea]||OSC_CATEGORY_COLORS[p.areas?.[0]]||'#8a9aa8';
      const terr=p.t||'—';
      const terrCol=terr==='Grande Mucuripe'?UI_COLORS.territoryGM:UI_COLORS.territoryGBJ;
      const areaChips=(p.areas||[]).map(a=>`<span style="display:inline-block;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:3px;font-size:10px;font-weight:600;background:${OSC_CATEGORY_COLORS[a]||'#8a9aa8'};color:#fff;margin:1px 2px">${OSC_AREA_SHORT_LABELS[a]||a}</span>`).join('');
      const mk=L.marker([p.plat||p.lat,p.plon||p.lon],{
        icon:L.divIcon({
          className:'',
          html:`<div style="width:10px;height:10px;border-radius:50%;background:${color};border:1.5px solid #fff;box-shadow:0 1px 4px rgba(0,0,0,.25)"></div>`,
          iconSize:[10,10],iconAnchor:[5,5]
        })
      });
      mk._oscData=p;
      mk.bindPopup(`<div class="mge-popup">
        <h4>${p.n}</h4>
        ${p.rf?'<div style="font-size:11px;color:#5C6E7E;margin-bottom:4px">'+p.rf+'</div>':''}
        <div style="font-size:10px;font-weight:700;color:${terrCol};margin-bottom:6px">${terr} · ${p.b}</div>
        <div style="display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:2px;margin:6px 0">${areaChips}</div>
        ${p.tipo?'<div style="font-size:11px;color:#5C6E7E;margin-top:4px">Tipo: <strong>'+p.tipo+'</strong></div>':''}
        <div style="font-size:10px;color:#999;margin-top:4px;border-top:1px solid #eee;padding-top:4px">${p.addr||''}</div>
      </div>`,{maxWidth:340});
      cluster.addLayer(mk);
    });

    m.fitBounds(MAPS['map-osc'].geo.getBounds(),{padding:[20,20]});
  }

  // --- Filter + render logic ---
  let oscListVisible=20;

  function getFilteredOSC(){
    const bf=selBairro.value;
    const tf=selTerr.value;
    const af=selArea.value;
    const sf=(document.getElementById('osc-search').value||'').toLowerCase();
    let filtered=[...OSC_POINTS];
    if(bf) filtered=filtered.filter(p=>p.b===bf);
    if(tf) filtered=filtered.filter(p=>p.t===tf);
    if(af) filtered=filtered.filter(p=>(p.areas||[]).includes(af));
    if(sf) filtered=filtered.filter(p=>p.n.toLowerCase().includes(sf)||(p.rf&&p.rf.toLowerCase().includes(sf)));
    return filtered;
  }

  function renderOscMap(){
    const cl=MAPS['map-osc'].clusterLayer;
    cl.clearLayers();
    const filtered=getFilteredOSC();
    filtered.forEach(p=>{
      const primaryArea=p.primary||'Sem classificação';
      const color=OSC_CATEGORY_COLORS[primaryArea]||OSC_CATEGORY_COLORS[p.areas?.[0]]||'#8a9aa8';
      const terr=p.t||'—';
      const terrCol=terr==='Grande Mucuripe'?UI_COLORS.territoryGM:UI_COLORS.territoryGBJ;
      const areaChips=(p.areas||[]).map(a=>`<span style="display:inline-block;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:3px;font-size:10px;font-weight:600;background:${OSC_CATEGORY_COLORS[a]||'#8a9aa8'};color:#fff;margin:1px 2px">${OSC_AREA_SHORT_LABELS[a]||a}</span>`).join('');
      const mk=L.marker([p.plat||p.lat,p.plon||p.lon],{
        icon:L.divIcon({
          className:'',
          html:`<div style="width:10px;height:10px;border-radius:50%;background:${color};border:1.5px solid #fff;box-shadow:0 1px 4px rgba(0,0,0,.25)"></div>`,
          iconSize:[10,10],iconAnchor:[5,5]
        })
      });
      mk.bindPopup(`<div class="mge-popup">
        <h4>${p.n}</h4>
        ${p.rf?'<div style="font-size:11px;color:#5C6E7E;margin-bottom:4px">'+p.rf+'</div>':''}
        <div style="font-size:10px;font-weight:700;color:${terrCol};margin-bottom:6px">${terr} · ${p.b}</div>
        <div style="display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:2px;margin:6px 0">${areaChips}</div>
        ${p.tipo?'<div style="font-size:11px;color:#5C6E7E;margin-top:4px">Tipo: <strong>'+p.tipo+'</strong></div>':''}
        <div style="font-size:10px;color:#999;margin-top:4px;border-top:1px solid #eee;padding-top:4px">${p.addr||''}</div>
      </div>`,{maxWidth:340});
      cl.addLayer(mk);
    });

    document.getElementById('osc-map-count').textContent=filtered.length+' OSCs';
    oscListVisible=20;
    renderOscList();
  }

  function renderOscList(){
    const filtered=getFilteredOSC();
    const bf=selBairro.value,tf=selTerr.value,af=selArea.value;
    const sf=(document.getElementById('osc-search').value||'').toLowerCase();
    const activeFilters=[bf&&`bairro: ${bf}`,tf&&`território: ${tf}`,af&&`área: ${OSC_AREA_SHORT_LABELS[af]||af}`,sf&&`busca: "${sf}"`].filter(Boolean);

    const filteredAreaCounts={};
    filtered.forEach(p=>{(p.areas||[]).forEach(a=>{filteredAreaCounts[a]=(filteredAreaCounts[a]||0)+1;})});
    const areaBreakdown=Object.entries(filteredAreaCounts).sort((a,b)=>b[1]-a[1]).slice(0,3).map(([k,v])=>`${OSC_AREA_SHORT_LABELS[k]||k}: ${v}`).join(' · ');

    document.getElementById('osc-analysis-text').innerHTML=`<strong>${filtered.length}</strong> OSC${filtered.length!==1?'s':''}${activeFilters.length?` <span style="color:var(--brand)">(${activeFilters.join(' · ')})</span>`:''}<br><span style="font-size:10px;color:var(--muted)">${areaBreakdown}</span>`;

    const shown=filtered.slice(0,oscListVisible);
    const listEl=document.getElementById('osc-top-list');
    listEl.innerHTML=shown.map((p,i)=>{
      const terrCol=p.t==='Grande Mucuripe'?UI_COLORS.territoryGM:UI_COLORS.territoryGBJ;
      const primaryArea=p.primary||p.areas?.[0]||'Sem classificação';
      const areaCol=OSC_CATEGORY_COLORS[primaryArea]||'#8a9aa8';
      return `<div style="padding:5px 0;border-bottom:1px solid var(--line-2)">
        <div style="display:flex;gap:6px;align-items:center">
          <span style="font-weight:600;color:var(--muted);font-size:10px;min-width:18px">${i+1}</span>
          <span style="width:8px;height:8px;border-radius:50%;background:${areaCol};flex-shrink:0"></span>
          <span style="flex:1;font-size:11px;font-weight:500;color:#0B2B44;line-height:1.3">${p.n}</span>
        </div>
        <div style="padding-left:32px;margin-top:2px;display:flex;gap:4px;flex-wrap:wrap;align-items:center">
          <span style="width:6px;height:6px;border-radius:50%;background:${terrCol};display:inline-block;flex-shrink:0"></span>
          <span style="font-size:10px;color:var(--muted)">${p.b} · ${p.t}</span>
          <span style="font-size:9px;padding:1px 5px;border-radius:3px;background:#f3f6fb;color:#4f7b98">${OSC_AREA_SHORT_LABELS[primaryArea]||primaryArea}</span>
        </div>
      </div>`;
    }).join('')+(oscListVisible<filtered.length?`<div style="text-align:center;padding:10px;font-size:11px;color:var(--muted)">Exibindo ${oscListVisible} de ${filtered.length} — role para ver mais ↓</div>`:'');
  }

  // Lazy scroll
  document.getElementById('osc-top-list').addEventListener('scroll',function(){
    if(this.scrollTop+this.clientHeight>=this.scrollHeight-30){
      const total=getFilteredOSC().length;
      if(oscListVisible<total){
        oscListVisible=Math.min(oscListVisible+20,total);
        renderOscList();
      }
    }
  });

  function updateOscFilterUI(){
    const bf=selBairro.value,tf=selTerr.value,af=selArea.value;
    const sf=(document.getElementById('osc-search').value||'').toLowerCase();
    const active=[bf,tf,af,sf].filter(Boolean).length;
    document.getElementById('osc-clear-filters').style.display=active?'':'none';
    const badge=document.getElementById('osc-filter-count');
    badge.style.display=active?'':'none';
    if(active) badge.textContent=`${active} filtro${active>1?'s':''}`;
    renderOscMap();
  }

  renderOscMap();
  selBairro.addEventListener('change',updateOscFilterUI);
  selTerr.addEventListener('change',updateOscFilterUI);
  selArea.addEventListener('change',updateOscFilterUI);
  document.getElementById('osc-search').addEventListener('input',updateOscFilterUI);
  document.getElementById('osc-clear-filters').addEventListener('click',()=>{
    selBairro.value='';selTerr.value='';selArea.value='';
    document.getElementById('osc-search').value='';
    updateOscFilterUI();
  });

  setTimeout(()=>{if(MAPS['map-osc']&&MAPS['map-osc'].map)MAPS['map-osc'].map.invalidateSize();},200);

  // ── OSC charts: por área e por bairro ──
  const areaCounts={};
  OSC_POINTS.forEach(p=>{(p.areas||[]).forEach(a=>{if(a&&a!=='Sem classificação')areaCounts[a]=(areaCounts[a]||0)+1;})});
  const areaEntries=Object.entries(areaCounts).sort((a,b)=>b[1]-a[1]);
  if(areaEntries.length){
    destroyChart('chart-osc-area');
    const cArea=document.getElementById('chart-osc-area');
    if(cArea){
      const h=Math.max(areaEntries.length*28,200);
      cArea.style.height=h+'px';
      cArea.parentElement.style.height='auto';
      CHARTS['chart-osc-area']=new Chart(cArea,{type:'bar',data:{labels:areaEntries.map(e=>e[0].length>40?e[0].slice(0,40)+'…':e[0]),datasets:[{data:areaEntries.map(e=>e[1]),backgroundColor:'rgba(11,79,168,.7)',borderRadius:3}]},options:{indexAxis:'y',responsive:true,maintainAspectRatio:false,plugins:{legend:{display:false},tooltip:{callbacks:{label:ctx=>ctx.raw+' OSCs'}}},scales:{x:{grid:{display:false}},y:{grid:{display:false},ticks:{font:{size:11}}}}}});
    }
  }
  const bairroCounts={};
  OSC_POINTS.forEach(p=>{bairroCounts[p.b]=(bairroCounts[p.b]||0)+1;});
  const bairroEntries=Object.entries(bairroCounts).sort((a,b)=>b[1]-a[1]);
  if(bairroEntries.length){
    destroyChart('chart-osc-bairro');
    const cBairro=document.getElementById('chart-osc-bairro');
    if(cBairro){
      const h=Math.max(bairroEntries.length*28,200);
      cBairro.style.height=h+'px';
      cBairro.parentElement.style.height='auto';
      const terrMap={};
      OSC_POINTS.forEach(p=>{if(!terrMap[p.b])terrMap[p.b]=p.t;});
      CHARTS['chart-osc-bairro']=new Chart(cBairro,{type:'bar',data:{labels:bairroEntries.map(e=>e[0]),datasets:[{data:bairroEntries.map(e=>e[1]),backgroundColor:bairroEntries.map(e=>terrMap[e[0]]==='Grande Mucuripe'?'rgba(11,79,168,.75)':'rgba(30,64,175,.55)'),borderRadius:3}]},options:{indexAxis:'y',responsive:true,maintainAspectRatio:false,plugins:{legend:{display:false},tooltip:{callbacks:{label:ctx=>ctx.raw+' OSCs'}}},scales:{x:{grid:{display:false}},y:{grid:{display:false},ticks:{font:{size:11}}}}}});
    }
  }

  // ── Tabela completa de OSCs ──
  let oscTableSort='nome',oscTableDir=-1;
  function renderOscTable(){
    const q=(document.getElementById('osc-table-search').value||'').toLowerCase().trim();
    const tf=document.getElementById('osc-table-terr').value;
    const af=document.getElementById('osc-table-area').value;
    let rows=[...OSC_POINTS];
    if(q) rows=rows.filter(r=>(r.n||'').toLowerCase().includes(q)||(r.rf||'').toLowerCase().includes(q)||(r.b||'').toLowerCase().includes(q));
    if(tf) rows=rows.filter(r=>r.t===tf);
    if(af) rows=rows.filter(r=>(r.areas||[]).includes(af));
    rows.sort((a,b)=>{
      let av,bv;
      if(oscTableSort==='nome'){av=(a.n||'').toLowerCase();bv=(b.n||'').toLowerCase();}
      else if(oscTableSort==='bairro'){av=(a.b||'').toLowerCase();bv=(b.b||'').toLowerCase();}
      else if(oscTableSort==='area'){av=(a.areas||[])[0]||'';bv=(b.areas||[])[0]||'';}
      else if(oscTableSort==='terr'){av=(a.t||'').toLowerCase();bv=(b.t||'').toLowerCase();}
      else{av=a[oscTableSort]||0;bv=b[oscTableSort]||0;}
      if(typeof av==='number'&&typeof bv==='number') return oscTableDir===-1?bv-av:av-bv;
      return oscTableDir===-1?String(bv).localeCompare(String(av),'pt'):String(av).localeCompare(String(bv),'pt');
    });
    const countEl=document.getElementById('osc-table-count');
    if(countEl) countEl.textContent=rows.length+' de '+OSC_POINTS.length+' organizações';
    const cols=[{k:'nome',label:'Organização'},{k:'bairro',label:'Bairro'},{k:'area',label:'Área de atuação'},{k:'terr',label:'Território'}];
    const tbl=document.getElementById('table-osc-list');
    if(!tbl) return;
    const terrColors={'Grande Mucuripe':'#0B4FA8','Grande Bom Jardim':'#1E40AF'};
    tbl.innerHTML=`<thead><tr>${cols.map(c=>`<th class="sortable${oscTableSort===c.k?' sort-'+(oscTableDir===1?'asc':'desc'):''}" data-osc-col="${c.k}">${c.label}<span class="sort-icon">${oscTableSort===c.k?(oscTableDir===1?'↑':'↓'):'↕'}</span></th>`).join('')}</tr></thead><tbody>${rows.slice(0,500).map(r=>{
      const tCol=terrColors[r.t]||'#999';
      const nomeTitle=[r.n,r.rf].filter(Boolean).join(' · ');
      return `<tr><td style="font-weight:600;max-width:300px;overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis;white-space:nowrap" title="${nomeTitle}">${r.n||r.rf||'—'}</td><td>${r.b||'—'}</td><td>${(r.areas||[]).filter(a=>a!=='Sem classificação').map(a=>`<span class="tag" style="margin:1px 2px">${a.length>30?a.slice(0,30)+'…':a}</span>`).join(' ')||'—'}</td><td><span class="region-dot" style="background:${tCol};margin-right:4px"></span>${r.t||'—'}</td></tr>`;
    }).join('')}${rows.length>500?`<tr><td colspan="4" style="text-align:center;color:var(--muted);font-size:12px;padding:12px">Mostrando 500 de ${rows.length} organizações.</td></tr>`:''}</tbody>`;
    document.querySelectorAll('#table-osc-list th.sortable').forEach(th=>{
      th.onclick=()=>{
        const col=th.dataset.oscCol;
        if(oscTableSort===col) oscTableDir*=-1;
        else{oscTableSort=col;oscTableDir=-1;}
        renderOscTable();
      };
    });
  }
  // Populate area filter for table
  const oscTableAreaSel=document.getElementById('osc-table-area');
  if(oscTableAreaSel&&oscTableAreaSel.options.length<=1){
    const areas=[...new Set(OSC_POINTS.flatMap(p=>p.areas||[]))].filter(a=>a&&a!=='Sem classificação').sort();
    areas.forEach(a=>{const o=document.createElement('option');o.value=a;o.textContent=a;oscTableAreaSel.appendChild(o);});
  }
  renderOscTable();
  ['osc-table-search','osc-table-terr','osc-table-area'].forEach(id=>{
    const el=document.getElementById(id);
    if(el) el.addEventListener(el.tagName==='INPUT'?'input':'change',renderOscTable);
  });
}

function initNetworkFort(){
  /* ── 4 anchor organisations ── */
  const ANCHORS=[
    {id:'ccbj',name:'Centro Cultural Bom Jardim',short:'CCBJ',label:'CCBJ — Bom Jardim',
     bairro:'Bom Jardim',territory:'Grande Bom Jardim',color:'#E63946',
     focus:['Cultura e recreação','Educação e pesquisa'],
     related:['Desenvolvimento e defesa de direitos','Assistência social'],
     desc:'Equipamento cultural do Governo do Ceará, gerido pelo IDM/Secult. Primeiro espaço cultural público fora do corredor turístico de Fortaleza. Oferece formação em artes, dança, teatro, música e audiovisual pelo programa ECA. Atende 50 mil pessoas/ano no Grande Bom Jardim.'},
    {id:'msm',name:'Movimento Saúde Mental',short:'MSM',label:'Saúde Mental — Bom Jardim',
     bairro:'Bom Jardim',territory:'Grande Bom Jardim',color:'#7C3AED',
     focus:['Saúde','Assistência social'],
     related:['Desenvolvimento e defesa de direitos','Educação e pesquisa'],
     desc:'Fundado em 1996 pelo Pe. Rino Bonvini. Atua com saúde mental comunitária pela Abordagem Sistêmica Comunitária. O Giardino Buffet gera renda solidária para mulheres. A Escola de Gastronomia Autossustentável (EGA) forma profissionais no território.'},
    {id:'egsb',name:'Escola de Gastronomia Social Ivens Dias Branco',short:'EGS',label:'Gastronomia Social — Cais do Porto',
     bairro:'Cais do Porto',territory:'Grande Mucuripe',color:'#0B4FA8',
     focus:['Educação e pesquisa','Assistência social'],
     related:['Saúde','Desenvolvimento e defesa de direitos'],
     desc:'Gerida pelo IDM/Secult, inaugurada em 2018 no Cais do Porto. Centro de formação, pesquisa e inovação em gastronomia social. Oferece 300 vagas gratuitas por ciclo. Vencedora do Prêmio LED 2026. Parceira da Coalizão pela Alimentação Escolar.'},
    {id:'im',name:'Instituto Metamorfose',short:'IM',label:'Inst. Metamorfose — Cais do Porto',
     bairro:'Cais do Porto',territory:'Grande Mucuripe',color:'#D97706',
     focus:['Assistência social','Desenvolvimento e defesa de direitos'],
     related:['Educação e pesquisa','Saúde'],
     desc:'Nascido de um grupo de rap em Serviluz (2007). Atua com educação (programação, inglês), segurança alimentar (programa MetaMOHfome de resgate de alimentos) e turismo comunitário. Parceiro do Pacto Contra a Fome e Gerando Falcões.'}
  ];

  const anchorNodes=ANCHORS.map(a=>({
    id:a.id,name:a.name,shortName:a.short,label:a.label,desc:a.desc,
    focus:a.focus,related:a.related,area:a.focus[0],
    bairro:a.bairro,territory:a.territory,isAnchor:true,color:a.color
  }));

  /* ── Score each OSC against each anchor ── */
  const oscScored=[];
  const oscBestAnchor={};
  OSC_POINTS.forEach((o,i)=>{
    let bestScore=0, bestAnchorId=null;
    ANCHORS.forEach(a=>{
      let thematic=0,geographic=0;
      const primary=o.primary||'';
      const areas=o.areas||[];
      if(a.focus.some(f=>areas.includes(f)||primary===f)) thematic=3;
      else if(a.related.some(r=>areas.includes(r)||primary===r)) thematic=1;
      if(o.b===a.bairro) geographic=3;
      else if(o.t===a.territory) geographic=2;
      else if(o.t&&o.t!==a.territory) geographic=1;
      const sc=thematic+geographic;
      if(sc>bestScore){bestScore=sc;bestAnchorId=a.id;}
    });
    if(bestScore>=2){
      oscScored.push({idx:i,...o,score:bestScore,anchorId:bestAnchorId,
        territory:o.t||'',region:o.t||''});
      oscBestAnchor['osc-'+i]=bestAnchorId;
    }
  });

  const nodes=[...anchorNodes,...oscScored.map(o=>({
    id:'osc-'+o.idx,name:o.n,rf:o.rf,shortName:(o.n||'').slice(0,25),
    area:o.primary||o.areas[0]||'',areas:o.areas,primary:o.primary,
    bairro:o.b,territory:o.territory,
    addr:o.addr,cnpj:o.id,tipo:o.tipo,
    score:o.score,anchorId:o.anchorId
  }))];

  /* ── Edges ── */
  const edges=[];
  oscScored.forEach(o=>{
    ANCHORS.forEach(a=>{
      let thematic=0,geographic=0;
      const areas=o.areas||[];const primary=o.primary||'';
      if(a.focus.some(f=>areas.includes(f)||primary===f)) thematic=3;
      else if(a.related.some(r=>areas.includes(r)||primary===r)) thematic=1;
      if(o.b===a.bairro) geographic=3;
      else if(o.t===a.territory) geographic=2;
      else if(o.t&&o.t!==a.territory) geographic=1;
      const sc=thematic+geographic;
      if(sc>=2){
        const strength=sc>=5?'strong':sc>=3?'medium':'light';
        edges.push({source:a.id,target:'osc-'+o.idx,strength,value:sc,anchorId:a.id});
      }
    });
  });
  // Peer connections
  for(let i=0;i<oscScored.length;i++){
    for(let j=i+1;j<oscScored.length;j++){
      const a=oscScored[i],b=oscScored[j];
      if((a.primary===b.primary||a.areas[0]===b.areas[0])&&a.b===b.b)
        edges.push({source:'osc-'+a.idx,target:'osc-'+b.idx,strength:'peer',value:1});
    }
  }

  /* ── Anchor cards ── */
  document.getElementById('net-anchor-cards').innerHTML=ANCHORS.map(a=>{
    const cnt=oscScored.filter(o=>o.anchorId===a.id).length;
    return `<div style="margin-bottom:10px;padding:8px 10px;border-radius:8px;background:#f8fafb;border-left:3px solid ${a.color}">
      <div style="font-weight:600;color:var(--navy);font-size:11px;margin-bottom:2px">${a.short} · ${a.bairro}</div>
      <div style="font-size:10px;color:var(--muted);line-height:1.5">${a.desc.slice(0,120)}…</div>
      <div style="font-size:10px;color:var(--navy);font-weight:600;margin-top:4px">${cnt} OSCs conectadas</div>
    </div>`;
  }).join('');

  /* ── Stats ── */
  const areaCount={};
  oscScored.forEach(o=>{const a=o.primary||o.areas[0]||'';areaCount[a]=(areaCount[a]||0)+1;});
  const strongCount=edges.filter(e=>e.strength==='strong').length;
  const peerCount=edges.filter(e=>e.strength==='peer').length;
  document.getElementById('network-stats').innerHTML=[
    {label:'OSCs conectadas',value:oscScored.length,sub:'Por afinidade temática e/ou geográfica'},
    {label:'Conexões fortes',value:strongCount,sub:'Alta afinidade temática + proximidade'},
    {label:'Conexões entre pares',value:peerCount,sub:'OSCs conectadas entre si (área + bairro)'},
    {label:'Áreas representadas',value:Object.keys(areaCount).length,
     sub:Object.entries(areaCount).sort((a,b)=>b[1]-a[1]).slice(0,5).map(([k,v])=>(OSC_AREA_SHORT_LABELS[k]||k)+': '+v).join(' · ')}
  ].map(c=>`<div class="analysis-stat"><div class="analysis-stat-label">${c.label}</div><div class="analysis-stat-value">${c.value}</div><div class="analysis-stat-sub">${c.sub}</div></div>`).join('');

  /* ── Filters: anchor, territory, bairro ── */
  const anchorSel=document.getElementById('net-anchor-filter');
  ANCHORS.forEach(a=>{const o=document.createElement('option');o.value=a.id;o.textContent=a.label;anchorSel.appendChild(o);});
  const terrSel=document.getElementById('net-territorio-filter');
  TERRITORIES.filter(t=>t!=='Todos').forEach(t=>{const o=document.createElement('option');o.value=t;o.textContent=t;terrSel.appendChild(o);});
  const bairroSel=document.getElementById('net-bairro-filter');
  const allBairros=[...new Set(oscScored.map(o=>o.b))].sort();
  function populateBairros(terr){
    bairroSel.innerHTML='<option value="">Todos os bairros</option>';
    const list=terr?allBairros.filter(b=>{
      const tb=TERRITORY_BAIRROS['Grande Mucuripe']||[];
      const tg=TERRITORY_BAIRROS['Grande Bom Jardim']||[];
      if(terr==='Grande Mucuripe') return tb.includes(b);
      if(terr==='Grande Bom Jardim') return tg.includes(b);
      return true;
    }):allBairros;
    list.forEach(b=>{const o=document.createElement('option');o.value=b;o.textContent=b;bairroSel.appendChild(o);});
  }
  populateBairros('');
  terrSel.addEventListener('change',()=>{populateBairros(terrSel.value);bairroSel.value='';applyFilters();});

  /* ── Degree buttons ── */
  let activeDeg='all';
  document.querySelectorAll('#net-degree-bar .net-deg-btn').forEach(btn=>{
    btn.addEventListener('click',()=>{
      document.querySelectorAll('#net-degree-bar .net-deg-btn').forEach(b=>b.classList.remove('active'));
      btn.classList.add('active');
      activeDeg=btn.dataset.deg;
      applyFilters();
    });
  });

  /* ── Clickable legend ── */
  const legendAreas=[...new Set(oscScored.map(o=>o.primary||o.areas[0]||''))].filter(Boolean);
  const hiddenAreas=new Set();
  const legendEl=document.getElementById('network-legend');
  function renderLegend(){
    const sorted=legendAreas.slice().sort((a,b)=>(areaCount[b]||0)-(areaCount[a]||0));
    legendEl.innerHTML=
      ANCHORS.map(a=>`<div class="net-leg" style="cursor:default"><div class="net-leg-dot" style="background:${a.color}"></div><span class="net-leg-name" style="font-weight:600">${a.short} (âncora)</span></div>`).join('')+
      sorted.map(a=>{
        const off=hiddenAreas.has(a);
        const cnt=areaCount[a]||0;
        return `<div class="net-leg net-leg-toggle" data-area="${a}" style="cursor:pointer;opacity:${off?0.4:1}">
          <div class="net-leg-dot" style="background:${off?'#ccc':(OSC_CATEGORY_COLORS[a]||'#888')}"></div>
          <span class="net-leg-name">${OSC_AREA_SHORT_LABELS[a]||a}</span>
          <span class="net-leg-count">${cnt}</span></div>`;
      }).join('');
    legendEl.querySelectorAll('.net-leg-toggle').forEach(el=>{
      el.addEventListener('click',()=>{
        const a=el.dataset.area;
        if(hiddenAreas.has(a)) hiddenAreas.delete(a); else hiddenAreas.add(a);
        renderLegend();applyFilters();
      });
    });
  }
  renderLegend();

  /* ── D3 graph ── */
  const container=document.getElementById('network-graph');
  const W=container.clientWidth||700,H=container.clientHeight||620;
  const svg=d3.select(container).append('svg')
    .attr('width','100%').attr('height','100%')
    .attr('viewBox',`0 0 ${W} ${H}`)
    .style('border-radius','8px');
  const tooltip=d3.select(container).append('div').attr('class','net-tooltip');
  const infoCard=d3.select(container).append('div').attr('class','net-info-card');
  const g=svg.append('g');
  svg.call(d3.zoom().scaleExtent([0.2,5]).on('zoom',e=>g.attr('transform',e.transform)));

  function buildInfoCard(d){
    const color=d.isAnchor?d.color:(OSC_CATEGORY_COLORS[d.area]||'#64748b');
    // Nome principal: prefere n (nome curto); se vazio, usa rf (razão social)
    const mainName=d.isAnchor?d.name:(d.name||d.rf||'Organização sem nome');
    const subName=d.isAnchor?(d.label?`${d.label} · Organização Âncora`:'Organização Âncora')
                            :((d.name&&d.rf&&d.rf!==d.name)?d.rf:null);
    let body=`<h4>${escHtml(mainName)}</h4>`;
    if(subName) body+=`<div class="net-card-sub">${escHtml(subName)}</div>`;
    if(d.isAnchor){
      body+=`<div class="net-card-tags"><span class="net-card-tag" style="background:${color}">Âncora</span>`;
      if(d.focus) body+=d.focus.map(f=>`<span class="net-card-tag" style="background:${OSC_CATEGORY_COLORS[f]||'#888'}">${escHtml(OSC_AREA_SHORT_LABELS[f]||f)}</span>`).join('');
      body+=`</div>`;
      body+=`<div class="net-card-row"><div class="lbl">Território</div><div class="val">${escHtml(d.territory||'—')}</div></div>`;
      body+=`<div class="net-card-row"><div class="lbl">Bairro</div><div class="val">${escHtml(d.bairro||'—')}</div></div>`;
      if(d.related&&d.related.length) body+=`<div class="net-card-row"><div class="lbl">Áreas afins</div><div class="val">${d.related.map(r=>escHtml(OSC_AREA_SHORT_LABELS[r]||r)).join(', ')}</div></div>`;
      if(d.desc) body+=`<div class="net-card-desc">${escHtml(d.desc)}</div>`;
    }else{
      const tags=[d.area].concat((d.areas||[]).filter(a=>a&&a!==d.area&&a!=='Sem classificação')).filter(Boolean);
      if(tags.length) body+=`<div class="net-card-tags">`+tags.map((a,i)=>`<span class="net-card-tag" style="background:${OSC_CATEGORY_COLORS[a]||'#888'};${i>0?'opacity:.75':''}">${escHtml(OSC_AREA_SHORT_LABELS[a]||a)}</span>`).join('')+`</div>`;
      body+=`<div class="net-card-row"><div class="lbl">Território</div><div class="val">${escHtml(d.territory||'—')}</div></div>`;
      body+=`<div class="net-card-row"><div class="lbl">Bairro</div><div class="val">${escHtml(d.bairro||'—')}</div></div>`;
      if(d.addr) body+=`<div class="net-card-row"><div class="lbl">Endereço</div><div class="val" style="font-size:10.5px">${escHtml(d.addr)}</div></div>`;
      if(d.tipo) body+=`<div class="net-card-row"><div class="lbl">Tipo</div><div class="val">${escHtml(d.tipo)}</div></div>`;
      if(d.cnpj) body+=`<div class="net-card-row"><div class="lbl">CNPJ</div><div class="val" style="font-family:ui-monospace,monospace;font-size:10.5px">${escHtml(formatCNPJ(d.cnpj))}</div></div>`;
      if(d.anchorId){
        const anc=ANCHORS.find(a=>a.id===d.anchorId);
        if(anc) body+=`<div class="net-card-row"><div class="lbl">Vínculo</div><div class="val"><span style="display:inline-block;width:7px;height:7px;border-radius:50%;background:${anc.color};margin-right:6px;vertical-align:middle"></span>${escHtml(anc.short)} · score ${d.score}</div></div>`;
      }
    }
    return `<div class="net-card-accent" style="background:${color}"></div>`+
           `<button class="net-card-close" type="button" aria-label="Fechar">×</button>`+
           `<div class="net-card-body">${body}</div>`;
  }
  function showInfoCard(d){
    infoCard.html(buildInfoCard(d)).style('display','block');
    const btn=infoCard.node().querySelector('.net-card-close');
    if(btn) btn.addEventListener('click',ev=>{ev.stopPropagation();infoCard.style('display','none');});
  }
  infoCard.on('click',ev=>ev.stopPropagation());

  const sim=d3.forceSimulation(nodes)
    .force('link',d3.forceLink(edges).id(d=>d.id).distance(d=>d.strength==='peer'?60:d.value>=5?100:140).strength(d=>d.strength==='peer'?0.08:d.value>=5?0.2:0.05))
    .force('charge',d3.forceManyBody().strength(d=>d.isAnchor?-800:-60).distanceMax(400))
    .force('center',d3.forceCenter(W/2,H/2))
    .force('collision',d3.forceCollide().radius(d=>d.isAnchor?35:18).strength(0.9))
    .force('x',d3.forceX(W/2).strength(0.02))
    .force('y',d3.forceY(H/2).strength(0.02));

  const strokeColor={strong:'rgba(230,57,70,.3)',medium:'rgba(124,58,237,.15)',light:'rgba(100,100,100,.07)',peer:'rgba(100,100,100,.03)'};
  const strokeW={strong:1.5,medium:0.8,light:0.4,peer:0.2};

  const link=g.append('g').selectAll('line').data(edges).join('line')
    .attr('stroke',d=>strokeColor[d.strength]).attr('stroke-width',d=>strokeW[d.strength])
    .attr('stroke-dasharray',d=>d.strength==='peer'?'2,2':null);

  const nodeG=g.append('g').selectAll('g').data(nodes).join('g')
    .call(d3.drag().on('start',(e,d)=>{if(!e.active)sim.alphaTarget(0.3).restart();d.fx=d.x;d.fy=d.y})
      .on('drag',(e,d)=>{d.fx=e.x;d.fy=e.y}).on('end',(e,d)=>{if(!e.active)sim.alphaTarget(0);d.fx=null;d.fy=null}));

  const nodeR=d=>d.isAnchor?16:Math.max(3,Math.min(7,(d.score||1)*1));
  nodeG.append('circle')
    .attr('r',nodeR)
    .attr('fill',d=>d.isAnchor?d.color:(OSC_CATEGORY_COLORS[d.area]||'#888'))
    .attr('stroke',d=>d.isAnchor?'#fff':'rgba(255,255,255,.6)')
    .attr('stroke-width',d=>d.isAnchor?2.5:1)
    .style('cursor','pointer')
    .style('filter',d=>d.isAnchor?`drop-shadow(0 2px 6px ${d.color}80)`:null);

  nodeG.filter(d=>d.isAnchor).append('text')
    .text(d=>d.shortName).attr('text-anchor','middle').attr('dy','.35em')
    .attr('fill','#fff').attr('font-size','7px').attr('font-weight','800').attr('letter-spacing','.04em')
    .style('pointer-events','none');

  nodeG.filter(d=>!d.isAnchor).append('text').attr('class','net-label')
    .text(d=>(d.shortName||d.name||'').slice(0,20))
    .attr('dx',d=>nodeR(d)+4).attr('dy','.32em')
    .attr('fill','var(--text)').attr('font-size','7px').attr('font-weight','500')
    .style('pointer-events','none').style('display','none');

  /* ── Hover ── */
  nodeG.on('mouseover',function(e,d){
    d3.select(this).select('circle').transition().duration(150).attr('r',d.isAnchor?22:nodeR(d)*1.5);
    link.transition().duration(150).attr('stroke-opacity',l=>(l.source===d||l.target===d)?1:0.08);
    nodeG.transition().duration(150).style('opacity',n=>{
      if(n===d) return 1;
      return edges.some(e=>(e.source===d&&e.target===n)||(e.target===d&&e.source===n))?1:0.1;
    });
    const areaLabel=OSC_AREA_SHORT_LABELS[d.area]||d.area;
    const color=d.isAnchor?d.color:(OSC_CATEGORY_COLORS[d.area]||'#888');
    tooltip.style('opacity',1).html(
      `<strong>${d.name}</strong>`+
      (d.bairro?`<br>${d.bairro}${d.territory?' · '+d.territory:''}`:'')+
      `<br><span class="net-tag" style="background:${color};color:#fff">${areaLabel}</span>`+
      (!d.isAnchor?`<br>Score: ${d.score}`:''));
    const rect=container.getBoundingClientRect();
    tooltip.style('left',Math.min(e.pageX-rect.left+12,W-220)+'px').style('top',(e.pageY-rect.top-60)+'px');
  }).on('mouseout',function(e,d){
    d3.select(this).select('circle').transition().duration(200).attr('r',nodeR(d));
    link.transition().duration(200).attr('stroke-opacity',1);
    nodeG.transition().duration(200).style('opacity',1);
    tooltip.style('opacity',0);
  }).on('click',function(e,d){
    e.stopPropagation();
    showInfoCard(d);
  });

  // Clicking on the empty graph area closes the card
  svg.on('click',()=>{infoCard.style('display','none');});

  sim.on('tick',()=>{
    link.attr('x1',d=>d.source.x).attr('y1',d=>d.source.y).attr('x2',d=>d.target.x).attr('y2',d=>d.target.y);
    nodeG.attr('transform',d=>`translate(${d.x},${d.y})`);
  });

  /* ── Unified filter ── */
  function applyFilters(){
    const anchorFilter=anchorSel.value;
    const terrFilter=terrSel.value;
    const bairroFilter=bairroSel.value;
    const hasFilter=activeDeg!=='all'||anchorFilter||terrFilter||bairroFilter;

    nodeG.each(function(d){
      if(d.isAnchor){d._visible=anchorFilter?d.id===anchorFilter:true;return;}
      let vis=true;
      if(hiddenAreas.has(d.area)) vis=false;
      if(terrFilter&&d.territory!==terrFilter) vis=false;
      if(bairroFilter&&d.bairro!==bairroFilter) vis=false;
      if(anchorFilter&&d.anchorId!==anchorFilter) vis=false;
      d._visible=vis;
    });

    link.each(function(d){
      const src=d.source,tgt=d.target;
      let vis=src._visible!==false&&tgt._visible!==false;
      if(vis&&activeDeg!=='all') vis=d.strength===activeDeg;
      if(vis&&anchorFilter&&d.anchorId) vis=d.anchorId===anchorFilter;
      d._linkVis=vis;
      d3.select(this).attr('display',vis?null:'none');
    });

    if(activeDeg!=='all'||anchorFilter){
      nodeG.each(function(d){
        if(d.isAnchor) return;
        if(!d._visible) return;
        const hasLink=edges.some(e=>e._linkVis&&(e.source===d||e.target===d));
        d._visible=hasLink;
      });
    }

    nodeG.attr('display',d=>d._visible?null:'none');
    // Only show labels when filtered to a manageable count
    let visCount=0;
    nodeG.each(d=>{if(d._visible&&!d.isAnchor)visCount++;});
    const showLabels=hasFilter&&visCount<=80;
    nodeG.selectAll('.net-label').style('display',d=>(showLabels&&d._visible)?null:'none');

    // Re-heat simulation to spread out filtered nodes
    sim.alpha(0.4).restart();
  }

  anchorSel.addEventListener('change',applyFilters);
  bairroSel.addEventListener('change',applyFilters);

  /* ── Fullscreen territory/bairro filters (synced) ── */
  const fsTerr=document.getElementById('net-fs-territorio');
  const fsBairro=document.getElementById('net-fs-bairro');
  TERRITORIES.filter(t=>t!=='Todos').forEach(t=>{const o=document.createElement('option');o.value=t;o.textContent=t;fsTerr.appendChild(o);});
  function syncFsBairros(terr){
    fsBairro.innerHTML='<option value="">Todos os bairros</option>';
    const list=terr?allBairros.filter(b=>{
      if(terr==='Grande Mucuripe') return (TERRITORY_BAIRROS['Grande Mucuripe']||[]).includes(b);
      if(terr==='Grande Bom Jardim') return (TERRITORY_BAIRROS['Grande Bom Jardim']||[]).includes(b);
      return true;
    }):allBairros;
    list.forEach(b=>{const o=document.createElement('option');o.value=b;o.textContent=b;fsBairro.appendChild(o);});
  }
  syncFsBairros('');
  // Sync FS filters → main filters
  fsTerr.addEventListener('change',()=>{terrSel.value=fsTerr.value;syncFsBairros(fsTerr.value);fsBairro.value='';populateBairros(terrSel.value);bairroSel.value='';applyFilters();});
  fsBairro.addEventListener('change',()=>{bairroSel.value=fsBairro.value;applyFilters();});
  // Sync main filters → FS filters
  terrSel.addEventListener('change',()=>{fsTerr.value=terrSel.value;syncFsBairros(fsTerr.value);fsBairro.value='';});
  bairroSel.addEventListener('change',()=>{fsBairro.value=bairroSel.value;});

  /* ── Fullscreen legend (mirror of main) ── */
  const fsLegendEl=document.getElementById('net-fs-legend');
  function renderFsLegend(){
    const sorted=legendAreas.slice().sort((a,b)=>(areaCount[b]||0)-(areaCount[a]||0));
    fsLegendEl.innerHTML=
      ANCHORS.map(a=>`<div class="net-leg"><div class="net-leg-dot" style="background:${a.color}"></div><span class="net-leg-name" style="font-weight:600">${a.short} (âncora)</span></div>`).join('')+
      sorted.map(a=>{
        const off=hiddenAreas.has(a);const cnt=areaCount[a]||0;
        return `<div class="net-leg" data-area="${a}" style="cursor:pointer;opacity:${off?0.4:1}">
          <div class="net-leg-dot" style="background:${off?'#ccc':(OSC_CATEGORY_COLORS[a]||'#888')}"></div>
          <span class="net-leg-name">${OSC_AREA_SHORT_LABELS[a]||a}</span>
          <span class="net-leg-count">${cnt}</span></div>`;
      }).join('');
    fsLegendEl.querySelectorAll('.net-leg[data-area]').forEach(el=>{
      el.addEventListener('click',()=>{
        const a=el.dataset.area;
        if(hiddenAreas.has(a)) hiddenAreas.delete(a); else hiddenAreas.add(a);
        renderLegend();renderFsLegend();applyFilters();
      });
    });
  }
  // Patch main renderLegend to also update FS
  const _origRenderLegend=renderLegend;
  renderLegend=function(){_origRenderLegend();renderFsLegend();};
  renderFsLegend();

  /* ── Fullscreen toggle ── */
  const expandBtn=document.getElementById('net-expand-btn');
  const backdrop=document.getElementById('net-fs-backdrop');
  let isFullscreen=false;
  function toggleFullscreen(){
    isFullscreen=!isFullscreen;
    container.classList.toggle('net-fullscreen',isFullscreen);
    backdrop.style.display=isFullscreen?'block':'none';
    expandBtn.textContent=isFullscreen?'✕':'⛶';
    expandBtn.title=isFullscreen?'Fechar tela cheia':'Expandir visualização';
    document.body.style.overflow=isFullscreen?'hidden':'';
    // Sync FS filter state
    fsTerr.value=terrSel.value;
    syncFsBairros(fsTerr.value);
    fsBairro.value=bairroSel.value;
    // Resize SVG viewBox
    setTimeout(()=>{
      const nW=container.clientWidth,nH=container.clientHeight;
      svg.attr('viewBox',`0 0 ${nW} ${nH}`);
      sim.force('center',d3.forceCenter(nW/2,nH/2));
      sim.force('x',d3.forceX(nW/2).strength(0.03));
      sim.force('y',d3.forceY(nH/2).strength(0.03));
      sim.alpha(0.3).restart();
    },100);
  }
  expandBtn.addEventListener('click',toggleFullscreen);
  backdrop.addEventListener('click',toggleFullscreen);
  document.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{if(e.key==='Escape'&&isFullscreen)toggleFullscreen();});
}

function initComp(){
  // ─── Configuração (Fortaleza) ──────────────────────────────────────────────
  const GROUPS = ['Grande Bom Jardim', 'Grande Mucuripe'];
  const GROUP_COLORS = {
    'Grande Bom Jardim': UI_COLORS.territoryGBJ || '#1E40AF',
    'Grande Mucuripe':   UI_COLORS.territoryGM  || '#0B4FA8'
  };
  const GROUP_DESC = (typeof TERR_DESC!=='undefined') ? TERR_DESC : {
    'Grande Bom Jardim':'Maior escala populacional, pobreza mais contínua e forte aderência aos eixos de inclusão produtiva e segurança alimentar. Indicado para atuação com foco em escala e adicionalidade social.',
    'Grande Mucuripe':'Maior heterogeneidade interna, melhor base econômica média e densidade institucional mais alta. Indicado para estratégias focalizadas por bairro e maior articulação entre vulnerabilidade e oportunidade.'
  };
  const groupOf = b => {
    const r = DATA.find(d=>d.b===b);
    return r ? r.t : '';
  };

  const TABLE_INDS = [
    {key:'pop',      label:'População',        fmt:'int',   agg:'sum',   higher:null},
    {key:'epob',     label:'Ext. pobreza',     fmt:'pct',   agg:'wmean', higher:'ruim'},
    {key:'cad',      label:'CadÚnico',         fmt:'pct',   agg:'wmean', higher:'ruim'},
    {key:'anal',     label:'Analfabetismo',    fmt:'pct',   agg:'wmean', higher:'ruim'},
    {key:'esg',      label:'Rede de esgoto',   fmt:'pct',   agg:'wmean', higher:'bom'},
    {key:'rendaPC',  label:'Renda PC',         fmt:'money', agg:'wmean', higher:'bom'},
    {key:'meiShare', label:'% MEI',            fmt:'pct',   agg:'wmean', higher:null},
    {key:'emp',      label:'Empresas',         fmt:'int',   agg:'sum',   higher:'bom'},
    {key:'osc',      label:'OSCs',             fmt:'int',   agg:'sum',   higher:'bom'}
  ];

  const RADAR_INDS = [
    {key:'epob',    label:'Extrema pobreza',    transform:v=>v},
    {key:'cad',     label:'CadÚnico',           transform:v=>v},
    {key:'anal',    label:'Analfabetismo',       transform:v=>v},
    {key:'esg',     label:'Déficit de esgoto',   transform:v=>1-(v||0)},
    {key:'rendaPC', label:'Déficit de renda',    transform:v=>Math.max(0,1-(v||0)/3000)},
    {key:'meiShare',label:'Informalidade (MEI)', transform:v=>v||0},
    {key:'raca',    label:'Pop. não-branca',     transform:v=>v},
    {key:'mulh',    label:'Chefia feminina',     transform:v=>v}
  ];

  // ─── Agregadores ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  function aggregate(rows, key, mode){
    if(mode==='sum') return rows.reduce((s,r)=>s+(+r[key]||0),0);
    const totPop = rows.reduce((s,r)=>s+(+r.pop||0),0);
    if(!totPop) return null;
    return rows.reduce((s,r)=>s+((+r[key]||0)*(+r.pop||0)),0)/totPop;
  }

  const groupAgg = {};
  GROUPS.forEach(g=>{
    const rows = DATA.filter(r=>r.t===g);
    const pop = aggregate(rows,'pop','sum');
    const values = {};
    TABLE_INDS.forEach(ind=>{ values[ind.key] = aggregate(rows, ind.key, ind.agg); });
    RADAR_INDS.forEach(ind=>{
      const raw = aggregate(rows, ind.key, 'wmean');
      values['rdr_'+ind.key] = ind.transform(raw);
    });
    groupAgg[g] = {rows, pop, bairros:rows.length, values};
  });

  // Totais (apenas Redes Ceará — 13 bairros acompanhados)
  const muni = {pop: aggregate(DATA,'pop','sum'), values:{}};
  TABLE_INDS.forEach(ind=>{ muni.values[ind.key] = aggregate(DATA, ind.key, ind.agg); });
  RADAR_INDS.forEach(ind=>{
    const raw = aggregate(DATA, ind.key, 'wmean');
    muni.values['rdr_'+ind.key] = ind.transform(raw);
  });

  // ─── 1) Mapa de divisão territorial ────────────────────────────────────────
  function renderDivisionMap(){
    const containerId = 'synth-division-map';
    const el = document.getElementById(containerId);
    if(!el) return;
    // Remove instância anterior se existir (reentrar na aba)
    if(MAPS[containerId] && MAPS[containerId].map){
      try{ MAPS[containerId].map.remove(); }catch(e){}
      delete MAPS[containerId];
    }
    const map = L.map(containerId, {zoomControl:false, attributionControl:true, scrollWheelZoom:true});
    MAPS[containerId] = {map};
    map.attributionControl.setPrefix(false);
    L.control.zoom({position:'bottomleft'}).addTo(map);
    L.tileLayer('https://{s}.basemaps.cartocdn.com/light_nolabels/{z}/{x}/{y}{r}.png', {
      subdomains:'abcd', maxZoom:19,
      attribution:'&copy; OpenStreetMap contributors &copy; CARTO'
    }).addTo(map);
    if(typeof GEOJSON==='undefined' || !GEOJSON.features) return;

    // Normaliza nomes (sem acento, minúsculas, sem conectores "do/da/de") para casar
    // variações entre DATA e GEOJSON — ex.: "Vicente Pinzon" vs "Vicente Pinzón",
    // "Praia Futuro I" vs "Praia do Futuro I".
    const normBairro = s => (s||'').normalize('NFD').replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g,'')
      .toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g,' ').replace(/\b(do|da|de|dos|das)\b/g,' ')
      .replace(/\s+/g,' ').trim();
    const dataBairroByNorm = new Map(DATA.map(d=>[normBairro(d.b), d.b]));
    const matchBairro = nome => dataBairroByNorm.get(normBairro(nome)) || null;

    const layer = L.geoJSON(GEOJSON, {
      style: feat => {
        const hit = matchBairro(feat.properties.nome);
        if(!hit) return {fillColor:'#F3F4F6', fillOpacity:0.35, color:'#D1D5DB', weight:.5};
        const terr = feat.properties.territorio || groupOf(hit);
        return {fillColor:GROUP_COLORS[terr]||'#888', fillOpacity:0.7, color:'#fff', weight:1.2};
      },
      onEachFeature: (feat, lyr) => {
        const hit = matchBairro(feat.properties.nome);
        if(!hit) return;
        const terr = feat.properties.territorio || groupOf(hit);
        const row = DATA.find(d=>d.b===hit);
        const pop = row ? row.pop : 0;
        lyr.bindTooltip(`<div style="font-size:11px"><strong>${hit}</strong><br><span style="color:${GROUP_COLORS[terr]||'#888'};font-weight:700">${terr||'—'}</span><br>Pop: ${fmt(pop,'int')}</div>`, {sticky:true, direction:'top', opacity:.92});
        lyr.on('mouseover', function(){this.setStyle({weight:2.6, fillOpacity:.85})});
        lyr.on('mouseout', function(){layer.resetStyle(this)});
      }
    }).addTo(map);

    // Fit to Redes Ceará bounds only
    try{
      const coalFeats = GEOJSON.features.filter(f=>matchBairro(f.properties.nome));
      const coalLayer = L.geoJSON({type:'FeatureCollection', features:coalFeats});
      map.fitBounds(coalLayer.getBounds(), {padding:[20,20]});
    }catch(e){ try{ map.fitBounds(layer.getBounds()); }catch(e2){} }
    // Força recálculo de dimensões caso a aba estivesse oculta quando o mapa foi criado
    setTimeout(()=>{ try{ map.invalidateSize(); }catch(e){} }, 80);
  }

  // ─── 2) Cards por território ───────────────────────────────────────────────
  function renderGroupCards(){
    const el = document.getElementById('synth-region-cards');
    el.innerHTML = GROUPS.map(g=>{
      const gd = groupAgg[g];
      const col = GROUP_COLORS[g] || '#888';
      const shareMuni = muni.pop ? (gd.pop/muni.pop)*100 : 0;
      const desc = GROUP_DESC[g] || '';
      return `<div style="background:#fff;border:1px solid var(--line-2);border-left:4px solid ${col};border-radius:8px;padding:12px 14px">
        <div style="display:flex;align-items:baseline;justify-content:space-between;gap:8px">
          <strong style="font-size:13px;color:var(--navy)">${g}</strong>
          <span style="font-size:10px;color:var(--muted);font-weight:600">${gd.bairros} bairros</span>
        </div>
        <div style="display:flex;gap:14px;margin-top:6px;font-size:11px;color:var(--navy);flex-wrap:wrap">
          <div><span style="color:var(--muted);font-weight:600">Pop.</span> <strong>${fmt(gd.pop,'int')}</strong></div>
          <div><span style="color:var(--muted);font-weight:600">%&nbsp;do acompanhado</span> <strong>${shareMuni.toFixed(1)}%</strong></div>
          <div><span style="color:var(--muted);font-weight:600">Ext. pobreza</span> <strong>${fmt(gd.values.epob,'pct')}</strong></div>
        </div>
        ${desc?`<div style="font-size:11px;color:#5C6E7E;margin-top:8px;line-height:1.5">${desc}</div>`:''}
      </div>`;
    }).join('');
  }

  // ─── 3) Tabela: indicadores consolidados por território (sortable) ─────────
  let regionSortKey = 'pop', regionSortDir = -1;
  function renderRegionTable(){
    const tbl = document.getElementById('synth-region-table');
    const hdrCols = [{key:'name',label:'Território',fmt:null,align:'left'}]
                    .concat(TABLE_INDS.map(i=>({key:i.key,label:i.label,fmt:i.fmt,align:'right'})));
    const arrow = k => `<span class="sort-icon">${regionSortKey===k?(regionSortDir===-1?'↓':'↑'):'↕'}</span>`;
    const headers = '<tr>'+hdrCols.map(c=>`<th class="sortable${regionSortKey===c.key?' sort-'+(regionSortDir===-1?'desc':'asc'):''}" data-k="${c.key}" style="text-align:${c.align};cursor:pointer;user-select:none">${c.label}${arrow(c.key)}</th>`).join('')+'</tr>';
    const sorted = GROUPS.slice();
    sorted.sort((a,b)=>{
      let va, vb;
      if(regionSortKey==='name'){ va=a; vb=b; }
      else { va=groupAgg[a].values[regionSortKey]; vb=groupAgg[b].values[regionSortKey]; }
      if(va==null && vb==null) return 0;
      if(va==null) return 1; if(vb==null) return -1;
      if(typeof va==='number' && typeof vb==='number') return regionSortDir===-1 ? vb-va : va-vb;
      return regionSortDir===-1 ? String(vb).localeCompare(String(va),'pt') : String(va).localeCompare(String(vb),'pt');
    });
    const body = sorted.map(g=>{
      const gd = groupAgg[g];
      const col = GROUP_COLORS[g] || '#888';
      return `<tr>
        <td style="text-align:left;font-weight:700"><span style="display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:6px"><span style="width:9px;height:9px;border-radius:50%;background:${col}"></span>${g}</span></td>
        ${TABLE_INDS.map(i=>`<td>${fmt(gd.values[i.key], i.fmt)}</td>`).join('')}
      </tr>`;
    }).join('');
    const muniRow = `<tr style="background:#F3F6FA;font-weight:700">
      <td style="text-align:left">Total acompanhado</td>
      ${TABLE_INDS.map(i=>`<td>${fmt(muni.values[i.key], i.fmt)}</td>`).join('')}
    </tr>`;
    tbl.innerHTML = `<thead>${headers}</thead><tbody>${body}${muniRow}</tbody>`;
    tbl.querySelectorAll('th.sortable').forEach(th=>{
      th.onclick = () => {
        const k = th.dataset.k;
        if(regionSortKey===k) regionSortDir = -regionSortDir; else { regionSortKey=k; regionSortDir = k==='name'?1:-1; }
        renderRegionTable();
      };
    });
  }

  // ─── 4) Tabela: comparativo por bairro (sortable) ──────────────────────────
  let sortKey = 'pop', sortDir = -1;
  function renderBairroTable(){
    const tbl = document.getElementById('synth-bairro-table');
    const hdrCols = [{key:'b',label:'Bairro',fmt:null,align:'left'},{key:'t',label:'Território',fmt:null,align:'left'}]
                    .concat(TABLE_INDS.map(i=>({key:i.key,label:i.label,fmt:i.fmt,align:'right'})));
    const arrow = k => `<span class="sort-icon">${sortKey===k?(sortDir===-1?'↓':'↑'):'↕'}</span>`;
    const headers = '<tr>'+hdrCols.map(c=>`<th class="sortable${sortKey===c.key?' sort-'+(sortDir===-1?'desc':'asc'):''}" data-k="${c.key}" style="text-align:${c.align};cursor:pointer;user-select:none">${c.label}${arrow(c.key)}</th>`).join('')+'</tr>';
    const rows = DATA.slice();
    rows.sort((a,b)=>{
      const va=a[sortKey], vb=b[sortKey];
      if(va==null && vb==null) return 0;
      if(va==null) return 1; if(vb==null) return -1;
      if(typeof va==='number' && typeof vb==='number') return sortDir===-1 ? vb-va : va-vb;
      return sortDir===-1 ? String(vb).localeCompare(String(va),'pt') : String(va).localeCompare(String(vb),'pt');
    });
    const body = rows.map(d=>{
      const col = GROUP_COLORS[d.t] || '#888';
      const cells = hdrCols.map(c=>{
        if(c.key==='b') return `<td style="text-align:left;font-weight:600">${d.b}</td>`;
        if(c.key==='t') return `<td style="text-align:left;font-size:11px"><span style="display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:5px"><span style="width:7px;height:7px;border-radius:50%;background:${col}"></span>${d.t||'—'}</span></td>`;
        return `<td>${fmt(d[c.key], c.fmt)}</td>`;
      }).join('');
      return `<tr>${cells}</tr>`;
    }).join('');
    tbl.innerHTML = `<thead>${headers}</thead><tbody>${body}</tbody>`;
    tbl.querySelectorAll('th.sortable').forEach(th=>{
      th.onclick = () => {
        const k = th.dataset.k;
        if(sortKey===k) sortDir = -sortDir; else { sortKey=k; sortDir = ['b','t'].includes(k)?1:-1; }
        renderBairroTable();
      };
    });
  }

  // ─── 5) Radar ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  function renderRadar(){
    destroyChart('chart-synth-radar');
    const canvas = document.getElementById('chart-synth-radar');
    if(!canvas) return;
    const axisMax = RADAR_INDS.map(ind=>{
      const maxV = Math.max(...GROUPS.map(g=>groupAgg[g].values['rdr_'+ind.key]||0));
      return Math.min(1, Math.max(0.1, Math.ceil(maxV*20)/20 + 0.05));
    });
    const labels = RADAR_INDS.map(i=>i.label);
    const datasets = GROUPS.map(g=>{
      const col = GROUP_COLORS[g] || '#888';
      return {
        label: g,
        data: RADAR_INDS.map((ind,i)=>{
          const v = groupAgg[g].values['rdr_'+ind.key] || 0;
          return (v/axisMax[i])*100;
        }),
        _raw: RADAR_INDS.map(ind=>groupAgg[g].values['rdr_'+ind.key]||0),
        borderColor: col,
        backgroundColor: col+'22',
        pointBackgroundColor: col,
        pointRadius: 3,
        pointHoverRadius: 5,
        borderWidth: 2
      };
    });
    CHARTS['chart-synth-radar'] = new Chart(canvas, {
      type:'radar',
      data:{labels, datasets},
      options:{
        responsive:true, maintainAspectRatio:false,
        plugins:{
          legend:{display:false},
          tooltip:{
            backgroundColor:'rgba(20,50,74,.94)',
            titleFont:{family:'Inter',size:12,weight:'700'},
            bodyFont:{family:'Inter',size:11},
            padding:10, cornerRadius:8,
            callbacks:{
              title:(items)=>items[0].dataset.label,
              label:(ctx)=>{
                const v = ctx.dataset._raw[ctx.dataIndex];
                return `${labels[ctx.dataIndex]}: ${(v*100).toLocaleString('pt-BR',{minimumFractionDigits:1,maximumFractionDigits:1})}%`;
              }
            }
          }
        },
        scales:{
          r:{
            min:0, max:100,
            ticks:{display:false, stepSize:25},
            pointLabels:{font:{family:'Inter', size:10.5, weight:'600'}, color:'#1B2D3E'},
            angleLines:{color:'#E5EAF0'},
            grid:{color:'#EEF2F6'}
          }
        }
      }
    });
    const leg = document.getElementById('synth-radar-legend');
    leg.innerHTML = GROUPS.map((g,i)=>{
      const col = GROUP_COLORS[g] || '#888';
      return `<span data-i="${i}" class="synth-leg-item" style="cursor:pointer;user-select:none;display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:6px">
        <span style="width:10px;height:10px;border-radius:2px;background:${col};display:inline-block"></span>
        <span style="font-weight:600;color:var(--navy)">${g}</span>
      </span>`;
    }).join('');
    leg.querySelectorAll('.synth-leg-item').forEach(el=>{
      el.onclick = () => {
        const idx = +el.dataset.i;
        const chart = CHARTS['chart-synth-radar'];
        const meta = chart.getDatasetMeta(idx);
        meta.hidden = meta.hidden===null ? !chart.data.datasets[idx].hidden : !meta.hidden;
        el.style.opacity = meta.hidden ? '.35' : '1';
        chart.update();
      };
    });
  }

  // ─── 6) Leitura sintética por território ───────────────────────────────────
  function renderReading(){
    const el = document.getElementById('synth-reading');
    const critIdxs = ['epob','cad','anal'];
    el.innerHTML = GROUPS.map(g=>{
      const gd = groupAgg[g];
      const col = GROUP_COLORS[g] || '#888';
      const critDiff = critIdxs.map(k=>{
        const v = gd.values[k]||0, m = muni.values[k]||0;
        return {k, v, m, dev:m? (v-m)/m : 0};
      }).sort((a,b)=>b.dev-a.dev);
      const worst = critDiff[0];
      const rendaPC = gd.values.rendaPC||0;
      const muniRPC = muni.values.rendaPC||0;
      const rendaDiff = muniRPC ? (rendaPC - muniRPC)/muniRPC*100 : 0;
      const esg = (gd.values.esg||0)*100;
      const labels = {epob:'extrema pobreza', cad:'CadÚnico', anal:'analfabetismo'};
      const worstTxt = worst.dev > 0.05
        ? `<span style="color:#991B1B;font-weight:700">${labels[worst.k]}</span> ${(worst.v*100).toFixed(1)}% (${(worst.dev*100).toFixed(0)}% acima do agregado)`
        : `indicadores críticos em linha com a média do recorte`;
      const rendaTxt = rendaDiff >= 10 ? 'renda per capita acima da média'
                       : rendaDiff <= -10 ? `renda per capita <strong>${Math.abs(rendaDiff).toFixed(0)}% abaixo</strong> da média`
                       : 'renda per capita próxima à média';
      const esgTxt = esg >= 90 ? 'saneamento adequado' : esg >= 70 ? 'cobertura de esgoto parcial' : `<strong>déficit sanitário</strong> (apenas ${esg.toFixed(0)}% com rede)`;
      return `<div style="background:#fff;border:1px solid var(--line-2);border-left:4px solid ${col};border-radius:8px;padding:12px 14px">
        <div style="font-size:13px;font-weight:700;color:var(--navy);margin-bottom:6px">${g}</div>
        <div style="font-size:11.5px;color:#1B2D3E;line-height:1.55">
          Concentra <strong>${fmt(gd.pop,'int')}</strong> habitantes em ${gd.bairros} bairros.
          Principal fragilidade: ${worstTxt}.
          ${rendaTxt}; ${esgTxt}.
        </div>
      </div>`;
    }).join('');
  }

  // ─── 7) Heatmap de vulnerabilidades ─────────────────────────────────────────
  function renderHeatmap(){
    const tbl = document.getElementById('synth-heatmap');
    if(!tbl) return;
    const dims = RADAR_INDS.map(i=>i.label);
    const maxPerDim = RADAR_INDS.map(ind=>{
      return Math.max(...GROUPS.map(g=>groupAgg[g].values['rdr_'+ind.key]||0), 0.001);
    });
    const thSort = 'class="sortable" style="cursor:pointer;user-select:none"';
    let html = `<thead><tr><th class="sortable" style="text-align:left;cursor:pointer;user-select:none">Território <span class="sort-icon"> ↕</span></th>`;
    dims.forEach(d => { html += `<th ${thSort}>${d} <span class="sort-icon"> ↕</span></th>`; });
    html += '</tr></thead><tbody>';
    GROUPS.forEach(g=>{
      const col = GROUP_COLORS[g] || '#0B4FA8';
      html += `<tr><td style="text-align:left;font-weight:700;color:var(--navy)"><span style="display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:6px"><span style="width:9px;height:9px;border-radius:50%;background:${col}"></span>${g}</span></td>`;
      RADAR_INDS.forEach((ind,i)=>{
        const v = groupAgg[g].values['rdr_'+ind.key] || 0;
        const norm = v / maxPerDim[i];
        const cls = norm >= 0.66 ? 'vuln-alto' : norm >= 0.33 ? 'vuln-medio' : 'vuln-baixo';
        const pct = (v*100).toFixed(1);
        html += `<td class="cell ${cls}" data-sort-value="${v.toFixed(4)}"><span>${pct}%</span></td>`;
      });
      html += '</tr>';
    });
    html += '</tbody>';
    tbl.innerHTML = html;
  }

  // ─── 0) KPIs resumo da Síntese ──────────────────────────────────────────────
  const kpiSynth = document.getElementById('kpi-synth');
  if(kpiSynth){
    const totalOsc = DATA.reduce((s,d)=>s+(+d.osc||0),0);
    const totalEmp = DATA.reduce((s,d)=>s+(+d.emp||0),0);
    const avgEsg = muni.values.esg;
    const avgCad = muni.values.cad;
    kpiSynth.innerHTML = [
      {label:'População acompanhada',value:fmt(muni.pop,'int'),sub:GROUPS.length+' territórios · '+DATA.length+' bairros'},
      {label:'Extrema pobreza média',value:fmt(muni.values.epob,'pct'),sub:'Ponderada por população (MDS, 2025)'},
      {label:'Renda per capita média',value:fmt(muni.values.rendaPC,'money'),sub:'Ponderada por população (IBGE, 2022)'},
      {label:'Cobertura de esgoto',value:fmt(avgEsg,'pct'),sub:'Média ponderada por população'},
      {label:'CadÚnico',value:fmt(avgCad,'pct'),sub:'Famílias registradas (MDS, 2025)'},
      {label:'OSCs + Empresas',value:fmt(totalOsc,'int')+' + '+fmt(totalEmp,'int'),sub:fmt(totalOsc,'int')+' OSCs e '+fmt(totalEmp,'int')+' empresas'}
    ].map(k=>`<div class="kpi"><div class="label">${k.label}</div><div class="value">${k.value}</div><div class="sub">${k.sub}</div></div>`).join('');
  }

  // ─── Execução ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  renderDivisionMap();
  renderGroupCards();
  renderRegionTable();
  renderBairroTable();
  renderRadar();
  renderReading();
  renderHeatmap();

  // ─── IPT: Índice de Priorização Territorial ────────────────────
  const radarSets = GROUPS.map(g => ({
    label: g,
    data: RADAR_INDS.map(ind => groupAgg[g].values['rdr_'+ind.key] || 0)
  }));
  const iptData = radarSets.map(s => {
    const avg = s.data.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0) / s.data.length;
    const cls = avg >= 0.70 ? 'Crítico' : avg >= 0.50 ? 'Alto' : avg >= 0.30 ? 'Moderado' : 'Baixo';
    const clsColor = avg >= 0.70 ? '#7F1D1D' : avg >= 0.50 ? '#854D0E' : avg >= 0.30 ? '#0B4FA8' : '#14532D';
    const clsBg = avg >= 0.70 ? '#FEE2E2' : avg >= 0.50 ? '#FEF9C3' : avg >= 0.30 ? '#EFF6FF' : '#DCFCE7';
    return { name: s.label, ipt: avg, cls, clsColor, clsBg, data: s.data };
  }).sort((a,b) => b.ipt - a.ipt);

  const iptLabels = ['Ext. Pobreza','CadÚnico','Analf.','Déf. Esgoto','Déf. Renda','Inform.','Não-branca','Chef. Fem.'];
  let iptSortKey = 'ipt', iptSortDir = -1;
  function renderIptTable(){
    const sorted = [...iptData].sort((a,b) => {
      const av = iptSortKey === 'name' ? a.name : (iptSortKey === 'ipt' ? a.ipt : a.data[parseInt(iptSortKey)]);
      const bv = iptSortKey === 'name' ? b.name : (iptSortKey === 'ipt' ? b.ipt : b.data[parseInt(iptSortKey)]);
      if(typeof av === 'string') return iptSortDir * av.localeCompare(bv,'pt');
      return iptSortDir * (av - bv);
    });
    const tbl = document.getElementById('synth-ipt-table');
    if(!tbl) return;
    const arrow = k => `<span class="sort-icon">${iptSortKey===k?(iptSortDir===-1?'↓':'↑'):'↕'}</span>`;
    const thStyle = 'cursor:pointer;user-select:none';
    tbl.innerHTML = `<thead><tr>
      <th class="sortable${iptSortKey==='ipt'?' sort-'+(iptSortDir===-1?'desc':'asc'):''}" style="text-align:center;width:40px;${thStyle}" data-ipt-k="ipt">#</th>
      <th class="sortable${iptSortKey==='name'?' sort-'+(iptSortDir===-1?'desc':'asc'):''}" style="text-align:left;${thStyle}" data-ipt-k="name">Território${arrow('name')}</th>
      <th class="sortable${iptSortKey==='ipt'?' sort-'+(iptSortDir===-1?'desc':'asc'):''}" style="text-align:center;${thStyle}" data-ipt-k="ipt">IPT${arrow('ipt')}</th>
      <th style="text-align:center;min-width:80px">Classificação</th>
      ${iptLabels.map((l,i)=>`<th class="sortable${iptSortKey===String(i)?' sort-'+(iptSortDir===-1?'desc':'asc'):''}" style="text-align:center;font-size:10px;${thStyle}" data-ipt-k="${i}">${l}${arrow(String(i))}</th>`).join('')}
    </tr></thead><tbody>${sorted.map((r,i) => `<tr>
      <td style="text-align:center;font-weight:700;color:var(--brand)">${i+1}º</td>
      <td style="text-align:left;font-weight:700">${r.name}</td>
      <td style="text-align:center;font-weight:800;font-size:16px">${r.ipt.toFixed(2)}</td>
      <td style="text-align:center"><span style="display:inline-block;padding:3px 10px;border-radius:4px;font-size:10px;font-weight:700;letter-spacing:.04em;background:${r.clsBg};color:${r.clsColor}">${r.cls}</span></td>
      ${r.data.map(v=>`<td style="text-align:center;font-size:12px">${v.toFixed(2)}</td>`).join('')}
    </tr>`).join('')}</tbody>`;
    tbl.querySelectorAll('thead th[data-ipt-k]').forEach(th => {
      th.onclick = () => {
        const k = th.dataset.iptK;
        if(iptSortKey===k) iptSortDir*=-1;
        else { iptSortKey=k; iptSortDir=-1; }
        renderIptTable();
      };
    });
  }
  renderIptTable();
  // Ensure sort icons appear on synth-heatmap after dynamic render
  if(typeof enableTableSorting === 'function') enableTableSorting();
}
function initTab(tab){
  if(INITED.has(tab)) return;
  if(tab === 'visao') initOverview();
  if(tab === 'demo') initDemo();
  if(tab === 'vuln') initVuln();
  if(tab === 'infra') initInfra();
  if(tab === 'econ') initEcon();
  if(tab === 'osc'){initOsc();initNetworkFort();}
  if(tab === 'comp') initComp();
  INITED.add(tab);
}
function activateTab(tab){
  document.querySelectorAll('.tab-btn').forEach(btn => btn.classList.toggle('active', btn.dataset.tab === tab));
  document.querySelectorAll('.section').forEach(sec => sec.classList.toggle('active', sec.id === `section-${tab}`));
  initTab(tab);
  if(MAPS[tab]) setTimeout(() => MAPS[tab].map.invalidateSize(), 120);
}
document.querySelectorAll('.tab-btn').forEach(btn => {
  btn.addEventListener('click', () => activateTab(btn.dataset.tab));
});

const TERRITORY_BAIRROS={'Grande Mucuripe':['Mucuripe','Cais do Porto','Vicente Pinzon','Vicente Pinzón','Papicu','Varjota','De Lourdes','Praia Futuro I','Praia Futuro II','Praia do Futuro I','Praia do Futuro II','Meireles','Aldeota','Cocó','Dionísio Torres','Cidade 2000','Edson Queiroz','Engenheiro Luciano Cavalcante','Guararapes','Salinas','Sabiaguaba','Manuel Dias Branco','Joaquim Távora','Aerolândia','Alto da Balança','Jardim das Oliveiras','Praia de Iracema','Tauape','Dias Macedo','Cidade dos Funcionários','Cambeba','Sapiranga / Coité','Parque Manibura','José de Alencar','Lagoa Redonda','Coaçu','Curió','Messejana','Paupina','Ancuri','Pedras','Jangurussu','Passaré','Barroso','Cajazeiras','Parque Iracema','Parque Dois Irmãos','Serrinha','Itaperi','Dendê','Parque Santa Maria'],'Grande Bom Jardim':['Bom Jardim','Siqueira','Canindezinho','Granja Portugal','Granja Lisboa']};

activateTab('visao');

function initFavelasMap(mun, bairrosGeo){
  const mapId='map-favelas-'+mun;
  const listId='favelas-list-'+mun;
  const munName=mun==='fortaleza'?'Fortaleza':'Maracanaú';
  const features=FAVELAS_DATA.features.filter(f=>f.properties.nm_mun===munName);
  const el=document.getElementById(mapId);
  if(!el||el._leaflet_id) return;
  const m=L.map(mapId,{scrollWheelZoom:true,zoomControl:false});
  L.tileLayer('https://{s}.basemaps.cartocdn.com/light_all/{z}/{x}/{y}{r}.png',{attribution:'© OSM · CARTO',maxZoom:19}).addTo(m);
  L.control.zoom({position:'bottomleft'}).addTo(m);

  // Territory colors for Fortaleza
  const TERR_COLORS={'Grande Mucuripe':UI_COLORS.blueSoft||'#93C5FD','Grande Bom Jardim':UI_COLORS.teal||'#2DD4BF'};

  if(bairrosGeo){
    L.geoJSON(bairrosGeo,{
      style:{fillColor:'#EEF4FF',fillOpacity:0.08,color:'#999',weight:0.8,opacity:0.4},
      onEachFeature:(feat,layer)=>{layer.bindTooltip(feat.properties.nome,{sticky:true,direction:'top',opacity:.8});}
    }).addTo(m);
  }

  // Find bairro for each favela via centroid point-in-polygon
  function getCentroid(feat){
    const coords=feat.geometry.type==='MultiPolygon'?feat.geometry.coordinates[0][0]:feat.geometry.coordinates[0];
    let lat=0,lng=0;
    coords.forEach(c=>{lng+=c[0];lat+=c[1]});
    return [lat/coords.length,lng/coords.length];
  }
  function findBairro(latLng){
    if(!bairrosGeo) return '';
    for(const feat of bairrosGeo.features){
      const coords=feat.geometry.type==='MultiPolygon'?feat.geometry.coordinates[0][0]:feat.geometry.coordinates[0];
      let inside=false;
      for(let i=0,j=coords.length-1;i<coords.length;j=i++){
        const xi=coords[i][1],yi=coords[i][0],xj=coords[j][1],yj=coords[j][0];
        if(((yi>latLng[1])!==(yj>latLng[1]))&&(latLng[0]<(xj-xi)*(latLng[1]-yi)/(yj-yi)+xi)) inside=!inside;
      }
      if(inside) return feat.properties.nome;
    }
    return '';
  }

  // Compute territory geographic centers for proximity fallback
  const terrCenters={};
  for(const [t,bs] of Object.entries(TERRITORY_BAIRROS)){
    const tFeats=bairrosGeo?bairrosGeo.features.filter(f=>bs.includes(f.properties.nome)):[];
    if(tFeats.length){
      let cx=0,cy=0,n=0;
      tFeats.forEach(f=>{
        const cs=f.geometry.type==='MultiPolygon'?f.geometry.coordinates[0][0]:f.geometry.coordinates[0];
        cs.forEach(c=>{cx+=c[0];cy+=c[1];n++});
      });
      terrCenters[t]=[cy/n,cx/n];
    }
  }

  // Enrich features
  const enriched=features.map(f=>{
    const [lat,lng]=getCentroid(f);
    const bairro=findBairro([lat,lng]);
    const bData=DATA.find(d=>d.b===bairro);
    let territory=bData?bData.t:'';
    if(!territory&&bairro){for(const [t,bs] of Object.entries(TERRITORY_BAIRROS)){if(bs.includes(bairro)){territory=t;break;}}}
    if(!territory){
      // Assign to nearest territory by geographic proximity
      let minD=Infinity,nearest='';
      for(const [t,center] of Object.entries(terrCenters)){
        const d=Math.pow(lat-center[0],2)+Math.pow(lng-center[1],2);
        if(d<minD){minD=d;nearest=t;}
      }
      territory=nearest||'Grande Mucuripe';
    }
    const tColor=TERR_COLORS[territory]||'#0B4FA8';
    return {feat:f, bairro, territory, tColor, bData, lat, lng};
  });

  // Group by territory
  const byTerritory={};
  enriched.forEach(e=>{
    const t=e.territory||'Outros';
    if(!byTerritory[t]) byTerritory[t]=[];
    byTerritory[t].push(e);
  });

  // Territory summary badges
  const summaryEl=document.getElementById('favelas-territory-summary');
  if(summaryEl){
    summaryEl.innerHTML=Object.entries(byTerritory).map(([t,items])=>{
      const col=items[0].tColor;
      return `<span style="display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:4px;padding:3px 8px;background:${col}20;border:1px solid ${col}40;border-radius:4px;font-size:11px;font-weight:600;color:#374151"><span style="width:7px;height:7px;border-radius:50%;background:${col}"></span>${t}: ${items.length}</span>`;
    }).join('');
  }

  // Draw polygons colored by territory
  const layerMap={};
  const gj=L.geoJSON({type:'FeatureCollection',features},{
    style:(feature)=>{
      const e=enriched.find(x=>x.feat===feature);
      const col=e?e.tColor:'#0B4FA8';
      return {color:col,weight:2,fillColor:col,fillOpacity:0.35,opacity:0.9};
    },
    onEachFeature:(feat,layer)=>{
      const e=enriched.find(x=>x.feat===feat);
      const bInfo=e&&e.bData?`<div style="margin-top:8px;padding-top:6px;border-top:1px solid #E5E7EB"><div style="font-size:10px;font-weight:700;color:#9CA3AF;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.05em;margin-bottom:4px">Dados do bairro ${e.bairro}</div><div style="font-size:11px;color:#5C6E7E;line-height:1.5">Pop. ${fmt(e.bData.pop,'int')} · Ext. pobreza ${e.bData.epob!=null?fmt(e.bData.epob,'pct'):'—'}<br>CadÚnico ${e.bData.cad!=null?fmt(e.bData.cad,'pct'):'—'} · Esgoto ${e.bData.esg!=null?fmt(e.bData.esg,'pct'):'—'}</div></div>`:'';
      layer.bindPopup(`<div style="padding:8px"><strong style="font-size:13px">${feat.properties.nm_fcu}</strong><div style="font-size:11px;font-weight:600;color:${e?e.tColor:'#0B4FA8'};margin-top:4px">${e?e.territory:''} · ${e?e.bairro:''}</div>${bInfo}</div>`,{maxWidth:280});
      layer.on('mouseover',function(){this.setStyle({fillOpacity:0.65,weight:3})});
      layer.on('mouseout',function(){gj.resetStyle(this)});
      layerMap[feat.properties.nm_fcu]=layer;
    }
  }).addTo(m);
  try{m.fitBounds(gj.getBounds(),{padding:[24,24]});}catch(e){}

  // Build interactive list grouped by territory
  const list=document.getElementById(listId);
  if(list){
    let html='';
    Object.entries(byTerritory).forEach(([territory,items])=>{
      const col=items[0].tColor;
      html+=`<div style="padding:8px 0 4px;margin-top:6px;border-bottom:1px solid #E8EDF2">
        <span style="display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:5px;font-size:10px;font-weight:700;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.06em;color:${col}">
          <span style="width:8px;height:8px;border-radius:50%;background:${col}"></span>${territory}
        </span>
      </div>`;
      items.forEach(e=>{
        html+=`<div class="favela-item" data-name="${e.feat.properties.nm_fcu}" style="display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between;gap:8px;padding:7px 8px;border-bottom:1px solid #F4F7F4;cursor:pointer;border-radius:4px;transition:background .15s">
          <span style="font-size:12px;color:#2A3B4C;line-height:1.3;font-weight:500">${e.feat.properties.nm_fcu}</span>
          <span style="font-size:10px;color:#9CA3AF;white-space:nowrap">${e.bairro}</span>
        </div>`;
      });
    });
    list.innerHTML=html;

    // Click to zoom
    list.querySelectorAll('.favela-item').forEach(item=>{
      item.addEventListener('mouseenter',function(){this.style.background='#EEF4FF'});
      item.addEventListener('mouseleave',function(){this.style.background=''});
      item.addEventListener('click',function(){
        const name=this.dataset.name;
        const layer=layerMap[name];
        if(layer){
          m.fitBounds(layer.getBounds(),{padding:[60,60],maxZoom:16});
          layer.openPopup();
          layer.setStyle({fillOpacity:0.7,weight:3});
          setTimeout(()=>gj.resetStyle(layer),2500);
        }
      });
    });
  }
}

/* ── Ordenação global de tabelas (1º clique = maior para menor) ─── */
function enableTableSorting(root){
  (root||document).querySelectorAll('table').forEach(tbl => {
    // Tabelas que possuem lógica própria de ordenação (re-render completo)
    if(tbl.id === 'table-econ' || tbl.id === 'table-mge-list' || tbl.id === 'table-osc-list' || tbl.id === 'synth-region-table' || tbl.id === 'synth-bairro-table' || tbl.id === 'synth-ipt-table') return;
    const ths = tbl.querySelectorAll('thead th');
    ths.forEach(th => {
      if(th.classList.contains('no-sort')) return;
      if(th.dataset.col !== undefined || th.dataset.econCol !== undefined) return;
      if(!th.classList.contains('sortable')) th.classList.add('sortable');
      if(!th.querySelector('.sort-icon')){
        const ic = document.createElement('span');
        ic.className = 'sort-icon';
        ic.textContent = ' ↕';
        th.appendChild(ic);
      }
    });
  });
}
function sortTableByColumn(tbl, idx, th){
  const tbody = tbl.tBodies[0]; if(!tbody) return;
  const rows = Array.from(tbody.rows).filter(r => !r.classList.contains('no-sort'));
  const curr = th.classList.contains('sort-desc') ? 'desc' : (th.classList.contains('sort-asc') ? 'asc' : null);
  const next = !curr ? 'desc' : (curr === 'desc' ? 'asc' : 'desc');
  tbl.querySelectorAll('thead th').forEach(h => {
    h.classList.remove('sort-asc','sort-desc');
    const ic = h.querySelector('.sort-icon'); if(ic && !h.dataset.col && !h.dataset.econCol) ic.textContent = ' ↕';
  });
  th.classList.add(next === 'desc' ? 'sort-desc' : 'sort-asc');
  const icon = th.querySelector('.sort-icon'); if(icon) icon.textContent = next === 'desc' ? ' ↓' : ' ↑';
  const parseCell = tr => {
    const c = tr.cells[idx]; if(!c) return '';
    const raw = (c.dataset && c.dataset.sortValue !== undefined) ? c.dataset.sortValue : c.textContent.trim();
    if(raw === '' || raw === '—' || raw === '-') return next === 'desc' ? -Infinity : Infinity;
    const cleaned = raw.replace(/\s/g,'').replace(/\./g,'').replace(',','.').replace(/[^\d.\-]/g,'');
    const num = parseFloat(cleaned);
    return (!isNaN(num) && cleaned !== '' && cleaned !== '-') ? num : raw.toLowerCase();
  };
  rows.sort((a,b) => {
    const av = parseCell(a), bv = parseCell(b);
    if(typeof av === 'number' && typeof bv === 'number') return next === 'desc' ? bv - av : av - bv;
    return next === 'desc' ? String(bv).localeCompare(String(av),'pt') : String(av).localeCompare(String(bv),'pt');
  });
  rows.forEach(r => tbody.appendChild(r));
}
document.addEventListener('click', e => {
  const th = e.target.closest('thead th');
  if(!th) return;
  if(th.classList.contains('no-sort')) return;
  if(th.dataset.col !== undefined || th.dataset.econCol !== undefined) return;
  const tbl = th.closest('table'); if(!tbl || !tbl.tBodies[0]) return;
  if(tbl.id === 'table-econ' || tbl.id === 'table-mge-list' || tbl.id === 'table-osc-list' || tbl.id === 'synth-region-table' || tbl.id === 'synth-bairro-table' || tbl.id === 'synth-ipt-table') return;
  const ths = Array.from(th.parentElement.cells);
  const idx = ths.indexOf(th);
  if(idx < 0) return;
  sortTableByColumn(tbl, idx, th);
});
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => enableTableSorting());
document.querySelectorAll('.tab-btn').forEach(b => b.addEventListener('click', () => setTimeout(enableTableSorting, 80)));
setTimeout(enableTableSorting, 600);
setTimeout(enableTableSorting, 1800);
</script>
</body>
</html>
